piier qey taio siil cheid chan saad keel daed chen
chieey keaid chaeay qaay oaiy chiar tao oeeil chieeo
qaeo ker tao oiael say kaaey oaad keel daed chiey
chaied diaiy kaaiao keear chao seaar sar seaao
saad tieer chaail cheao siio char kiiieo oir qar
saad qair kiiio diieo oead qier keiey qiio saay
daar chiiil daid tin oaen seiaed tein

shiair teed qeiaio chear teed qiil cheid oio chaan
chiel say qieo chao siien qeaal tiiay diel say tiy
kiid tiaeao oier kiid taal oiad chieer oeer diiin
qey teal siiio siiil tin oiid tiaeao oied kieer
sar seeeo qaar tied oair oiio saad teel oeeil
deer qar siad oeiy chiiay oiel chian saeay siead
oaiy chaar oeeen keo oeeao teio tiy kieel qiad
oen chaeay keeo oiiy der cheey oao chieo cheey oear
tiair kaiid chieed char ter chael seiair qaay siail
kaid deaar oir oiiy oaiio oeieeey oear cheear
tiieio taair saiao tear oaear kaaaad oiad chier
seiey cphaar chiiil daid tin oaen oeeo cheaer qeaal
tiiiir chaen oeo oeaiil chan chaied diaiy taar dio
seao saay cheeal oieo chaao oer kaeaen qaay siead
diiaay chiair teed qeiaio chear teed qiil cheid
deaar oaeay

shiair teed qeiaio chear qio siil chiel cheal oiy
keao dio oeean qeier qaad kaeieo sair keo oeel
deal sail chey kaiil qeeeer oiey oeal oiy qiey
diiil chaio chan oaer qaad kaeieo sair qeeaeo
qiier chaao cheey oeeeo qaar kaear tao chaaio qaeo
qey teaal keid chan saay sail saio dieo oean
chaiio seaay keay oeeaeo qiier seeel daal oaiir
chiaian tieel kaaaar cheay cheo oeel deal sail chey
qiead oeiey tiaiy

peaal tiiiir cheaid chaeay qeier qaad kaeieo sair
keeo saio dieo oeio oeen oaen kaiy oaaed keer
diaiy cphaio oeo chaain tieel kaaaar oiead oeiey
qey teed qeiaio chead qiio cheao chaio diao siin
siir chiid taal oiad deay seeiy daied diaiy kaear
qeao sead chieed oiy teal saaio daead sir qar
chaao oeil oiiien chiel chaaao chaaal kan saad
kaeieo sair teaar oeal oiey chiiay dian taao oieo
oeio oeen oaer qar oaen qaad kaeieo sair qeial
cheeiy oaiio seaay qar seaao saaio chain sear
qaiao tear chiy taieo oaiio sin seeeed daed oair
chiiio teer tieo chied diaen qail oiieo oiaid oer
kaear qaeo taed oiair oeal sein chiiaay chaeaay
qeio taed din chaiy oeial kaaaar oear tiaid taain
qeaad oaiid oer kaeiay qeain sar oaen qaad
kaeieo sair tiael qeeeer oaiao tear oaiio oiieo
dieo chey tair saair qail oiieo oiaid oer kieel
kiil dien oar chiael tin chaiy chaaiao keear seio
keao din oaeiay kaiiil say tieel kaaaar oein sio
sieaal chaan oeaid chaeay qeiey cphaar oeeeo qaar
kaear qaao oeao dad tiial oeio tain siiel chiiied
oeeo sir teaar oaaal chead kan sein sio sieaal
chaan chiar kir oaaao kiir tiair teal siiio siiil
tin oiid tiaeao oier tian qeial kaaaar oeaao
chead qay kir chaiy daieo oaiio oaein taio oead
qaid chey keeo sid kiy qiio cheied daal diay chaiy
cheiel kaeaer seil chiao seo chieo deeead chaan sein
qiio cheao chaeo chaan saiir dair oaiy oaear qiel
oeao deeead chaao chien oeeaeo qiier seiein ter
oao chieo cheey oeier teeo sieo ched kaael diao
kean chaail oaaaid siio char tiaian kaeay ckhiir
deeel oied qen chian chao chaieel oeen cheaad daal
dieey qeal chin oaaey diao qieo chan seier keeo
cheaed tien oeaao sieaal oeo sair qeeel daied
diaiy kaeay ckhiir chaaao kiir tiair taid qead keal
cheio tiy kiiy oaid tiao tey teeo sid qieo oiieo
chiey chieeen chieer chaiio cheel oiair siey diieio
taair sey tiaiy ctheer oaaal chead kan oiir chaio
chad kan siail qead kaieer cheil chiiiiy tiiay deer
chaiil taiy oeaid chaeay qeiey cphaar oeaao oiil
daear cheer oeieir seier qeaao sieaal oeo sair
teeo oiid kaey sar chio seao saay daiy chaaiao
keear seaid oied tair qar oiel chieo deeead chaan
oieed oaair keay oaad kaeieo sair keeo sid kiy
taeal tiaiar sier tian taao oieo oeio oeen seiey

shiair teed qeiaio chear tieeo chaed cheeay qiio
chien chail chey qeeid qiir oieo chao siien tiair
teed qeiaio chear tieeo chaed cheeay tieel kaaaar
chaen kail chey keer diiio teer taao oeil oiiien
chiel chaaao chaaal kan saad kaeieo sair qiaiy taao
cheey oar oaen tiael qeeeer oiio oieir kaeil
daael say kir seial qen chier teaeo kier seaao
saaio chaio oiy keal chaio oaaaid siin sar chial
oaeo chio oaio chiiied oeen oair tair oeaio deaao
seian taao oed tey qar chiael tin saiid deear
qiey diieio taair oaeo qiieo qay taiy chaaey
diead daid kear qeaal keay sieo sieo diao seian
kir chaiy oeiad tian qeial kaaaar oeaaal chey
qeiaey qial oaaaay teer cheeao siad daaiy tiiaay
siir oeial kaaaar chiir chiio siiin oaear taieo
oaiio sin seeeed daed oair chieeo cheeao oeao say
keil chiiiiy tian saiid deear qiio seeid kan
seeiiy oeer chaeil tiiiir chaen cheeo oeeen chiair
tieeo chaieel sio chear tieeo cheeao oeao say qeio
kiaey cheear kiao kaiio qeeey der sail oaio
chiiied oeen saiid deear qiey oiio chad qay kaeaen
qar seier teeo sieo ched kaael diao kean chaail
cheao siio char teed qiil cheid oaiy oaaey oiead
oeiey tier taiy chaaiao keear oir oair kaen qeaao
siad daaiy qiey diieio taair oaeil tiiiir oeaid
oied tair qieo sieo diao seian kaeay ckhiir chaaeey
oaen sieo chaid kier chiaiy

shiaid qay taieo oaiio oaein teeal sair taao cheey
cheeo oeid dead chey taio oeo chaain kaear taieo
deeeo chieed oiy teeal sair taao cheey cheeo oeid
dead chey qeiey cphaar oeeeo qaar kaear kiel chaaao
chaaal kan saad kaeieo sair kaen kiel chaed oeio
sin oeeo cheaer ter oao chieo cheey oeier qiiel
daio oeeo sir qeiein ter chieeo chaeaaal oen siio
saay deeeo oaad tey qar oaiy oaear kaeil oaid
oiiey teeal sair tiead daid kaid deaar oeil
chiiiiy ker qeio keeey qeao oed qen chiiaay siir
chaaao kiir keeo sial oaead tead saaiy cheed
qeiaio chear qiio chad qay taiy chaiio seaiid
oeieiy siio oieir kir cheal seao oaaaid siin oiir
daeaay tiy kiiy chaad dead kiao kiy tiieir qail
oeid ched teay seeaer kir saein oeieey qeier qaad
kaeieo sair keeo sid kiy tier tian kiid taal
oiaiir chiaiy

shiaid qay kaiio oeil oiiid tead oiy teeal sair
taio oeo chaain kaear kaiio oeil oiiid tead oiy
teeal sair taao cheey chail chey keer diaeio seeeer
daied diaiy kaear kiel chaaao chaaal kan siaiiiid
kael char qiey chaeil tiiiir chaiio oeid oay qeaad
oeaao oiy ter oer taao oiaid cheel chaey oiir
dieeo oaiiy kiiy saeal chaan saao oaeo keo chied
dial oaaaan oaeaen qeaaan oar chiael tin cheer
oeaao chieey tieeo oaiid chieeo chiaen qear tiaid
ker kiel chaaao seeeel kiy ter oaeil kan oiir
dieeel oiy qiio seeid kan oeead kaen qaay char
kao sieaad daio oeeo chey qeaaan oar oiel chaed
oein chier taao saiar qar chaao oeieeeed kiy ter
oaeo teao chaio diaeid qey ter chaail cheao siio
char kaeil tiiiir chaeaen tio seao chaal chiy ter
chieeo cheeao oeao chien oaay chaao cheey chiiio deed
kiy ter seay seier qeial dead saaiy kiiel chear
kieed cheel chaiiy kir oeil seaan ker qeio keeey
qeil oed qaio cheao oiair chiiaay siir oeiay kiid
qier kair tial oaeo chio oaio chiiied oeeo chey
tair chiao qaio dieo oeiaed kay qaay oeo cheel
deio qaeo qay kaaiad tien oail oeid daid kiey
chiieir qar seier qieo oaaeed tail chiy kiid taal
cheo saaaid oieo din saein oeieey qear qein teed
qiil cheieer saay seeiiy chaaen qaail oier taaio
siiil tin oaeay

faieo sir qeied kiy qied keieed chad kaen qaail
oier kaeay ctheao dio oeean tieao oer kaear taid
qiaiid oed qein teeal sair qaair tiao keeao qean
cheiey qiio saay chaear kiel chaaao chaaal kan cheeo
oaio deeal chey qar chieed dieen cheey cheed tear
kieed cheel chaiiy kir oaiiiy saar oeo chaieed
oaaaid siio oiy qiied qein ter chaied oaaaid siin
oiir chieed oaair keeo cheaer ter sail oeid diao
chan saar oier oeao chaal oiad oeo chaain kaaal
cheail ched tear sied kaear kir seo oaio oiiy
oaaao chaaio sied tair tiead daid kear qiir deeel
oied qen chian oead taed oiair cher oeal sein
chaail oaaaid siio seeeen chiael qeeeer chaeo taio
oen oiio chad qiieo oaein tael seeeey qein siio
oieir taaio seo chaed oaiid say kaaeey chaar oeaao
siaiiil chan oeeo oiiy oaaao chiid qay kir chiey
chiair teeal sair tiead daid kaid deaar oir oeao
seied qeiy chier kaiio seaay tair oiiio diieo
oead qier kir oaaan qeio kieo oeao chaeiad tiy
kiiy chao saal oaeo kiao kiy tiieid qean chiel
chaed oied teay seeaer kir oaear qaao chieo deeeo
chaaiay qaein oeieey qear qein teed qiil cheieer
seiey

peir kaiio qay kair qaair teeo saio oail chey
taio oeo chaain qaaaio dar tier kaead keean qail
oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kair qaair teeo saio oail
chey qeiey cphaar oeeeo qaar qiio oaiid cheieeer
cheal siiio siiil tin oiid tiaeao oied tan cher
sail oeid diao chan chier kir oaeil daaey chier
taao saiar qar chio seao saay chaear keeal deeead
chaao oer qeaid oied tair qar seeel daal cheo siin
sey tael oil teeay seaad oaiid oer qeaid oied
tair keer oaaal chead kan siad oaid tein qaiid
oiil oeaay tar taeal tiien oaen cheiey chiaeiy
tieeel oiy qiey diieio taair oaeiy keal chaio
oaaaid siin seeaer taar oaad qaio dain char kieed
cheel chaiiy tiel say taiy oaaaio dar kaiid dan
oer tieey kaaao kiir tiair tael oil teeay oaaiao
keear oir seo oaio oiiy oaaao chaaio sied tair
qeiein ter seay chio oeid daead sir teaay oay
qiead oio chear kaeo qiil chieer sar seeeo qaar
kaear taao saiad ken chaieo oaiio sin seeeed daed
oair cheey oaeil tiiiir chaaeeo chey qear qiey chaio
oead kan cheaar diiaan oiio siiin oiiy chaieo
oaiio sin sar oaiy oaear qaeo qaal chean cher
chiiaan cheey oao siad chaio daead sid teio tiy
kair qaay oeaio deaao chan cher cheey oao chieo cheey
oiail kiil chael char teey chian oeeed oiaiiir
chaaio seo chiiad qen oieey sey kaio oaiio kiy
kiiien chiiy oiier qaiiy saao seier kaiiin oaiiy
saao chaaan seier qeio oaein ter chiair teal siiio
siiil tin oeieeeed tial sir keay dieed dieen
chieeo oaiiy kir oaeil daaey siey oiio oieir kir
oaear kaiio seio keao cheeay taieo oaiio sin
saiil taed dio chan oao chieo cheey oein chier kaear
kieel kiil dien oar chair seied qiien qio sieaal
chiaen taaan kaiio oeieeel sin oieio qair cher
cheal siiio oaio diieo sir qar siiel daio oeeo
sir qaay siead diiaay chier qiian kiel chaaao seao
chaaio char keal sein chaail oaaaid siio seeeen
chiael qeeeer chaeo taio oen oiio chad qiieo oaein
tiiad taar diael qeeeer oeal oiy tiiay oeial qen
chaied diaiy qiio saay oeao chiiad tiaiy qeaao tey
keay chaiy chied chen oaead keean qeaao saad tieer
chiair qiio oieid kaen sied kaid tail saeo qey
kaeil tiiiir cheaid chaeay qar chieo oaaed tial
oain chiiieo oir qeeiin siail kay qial cheeeao
oiil diair qeeiy oeaal keay seeeo qaar kaear
qaao oeao dad tiial oeio tain siiel chiiied oeeo
sir kaen qeaaay siio oer kaaal chiy tier kaead
qir qail oeeeo chiiiil qay keil chiiiiy ker qiio
oed keao chiiiil tiieiy tiiaay cheey oer taaio seo
chieo oeao oen oiio siiin siian kaiio oiid qeaar
cheeo seio taeaad sir keaio chiiio cheo oeeer
oeeaeo qiier oaiy saeo qal kain chao siad chaio
daead sir qar seeiin siiel chiiied oeeo sir qiiio
oeel cher kiiy chao seaar siail kay

peir tiiil oaeid oaaiy qeaao tey keay deiad din
oaaed keer diaiy cphaio oeo chaain tiar qeaao tey
keay cheo cheel deio qaeo qay qeao oed qen chiieir
keay diaey tar qail oaio chiiied oeeo chey kear
qel qay tao cheal saiio oeen sar oail chan chael
oiiien diiay dieel qiey cher seay oiir diaeir
qiear qeaao sial saaad kier oiio chad taio saiio
sin chail oaen siiel chiiied oeeo oaaaan saeay
seiey

pio seeiid qair chiar qaao chieo deeeo chaaiay
kaeay ctheao dio oeean keo oaaeed kier seir kiio
seao oaaao diieiy qeaal tiiay daiy diel say
kaeay ckhiir chaaao kiir tiair teal siiio siiil tin
saao chieo deeeo chaaiay taiy oeaad oeaan seaao
chieey ter chieeey seier qial oaaer tio seao chaal
chiy ter chieeey oeay chiel chied kaeiar qeaid oied
tair qar siiid oair oair qeier qeaao sieaal oeo
sair qaay oeo oeear chaiy deal sead tiaeir kiio
siiin chaao oed tey qial chieen teer diao qiiiy
keeao siad daaiy tiieir teaar chaeil tiiiir cheaid
chaeay qeier tieo chied diaen qail oiieo dio sir
qar siiid daal daiy chaiy chair qieo oeel daed dio
oeean taaan kir oaaan taiy chieel kiil dien deaar
oiey chiio seao chiiel oiy tied qeio qay taaio
oeid oiiey qeeaer kir oaaan qieo dair teaay cheeo
chiair qeeiy cheao oier qeil chieo oiail qeeao oan
oear qiey chiio seao chiiel oiy teey seay chair
sieo oeel daed dio oeean qeeel oieal oid kier
cher saaao chaaio sied tair qar saay saair qaiil
qaal chain chiaiy siio saay oaao seier keeed
oiaiiir oiiy chaiy oaao chieo oaail say qeil chieo
dail oaio chiiied oeeo sen chiieir qeiey

shiair qio seeiid kiao kier seir keeeo qail sid
kiy keao dio oeean qeier keo oaaeed teio tear
oaer taaao kiel oed tey qeeeo qaar kaear kiel
chaaao chaaal kan chaaao qeil oer keiey qiio saay
chir chaiy oeaad oeaan seaao chieey ter chiael oiial
oen chaao saiar qar seeen qeier tieo chied diaen
qail oiieo oiaid oer taiy oeiay taaio oeid chiiey
tiieir tair sieo oeel daed dio oeean taaan kaaan
qiel chian saiiy saao seier kiiy oaaey chiir oaiao
tear oaiio oiieo oeeaey qaad teel oieo cheay sar
sieo oeel daed din oaaal cheail ched tear chio seao
saay oeaid oied tair qar siiel daio oeeo sir tio
seao saay chaen cheeo oeeen chiair tieeo chaieel sio
chear ter chieeey oeeo oaeied qen siied qein ter
chieeo oaieed chaeaey cheeeay daao cheey chiel chaal
deeead chaao say keil saaain chaaey chaiil taiy
oaaao kiir kaeo qiil cheid oio chaan oaaiao keear
oir seao sead oeian kiel cheeao cheied char qiey
chaeiy qiieo chaed cheeay kaaiar teey oer kaen
seial dead sen seay sar oaiy oeeal oieo chaao oer
qeaid oied tair keay oeeaeo qiier chiaey keo
oeaiil chan chiieir qar chial oaeo chio oaio dieo
din oir saiid deear qiey deaal teel oean oeiay
taieo oaiio sin seeaer kir oaiiiy sail chey qar
chiael tin oeaid chaeay qeier qail oeeeo oeeel qen
cheed qeiaio chear ter chiael oiiay oaen keeo cheaer
ter oaieo sir tieo chied diaen qail oiieo oiaid
oer kaear qaeo taed din oir seiay taiy chaaiao
keear oaeaen qieo sieo diao seian taiy chaaiao
keear seiay tao chaieel oeen seeaer kir seo oaio
oiiy oaar chaeaer chiy kaiio oeaiil chao oier kir
oaiiiy saar daao chiiad qen siied qein ter chiiin
tiair teal siiio siiil tin siiio kael char qiey
chaiid chaiin oaiio seaay qar seial cheeil chan siio
oieir kir oiid taal oiad deay siead oeiey tiair
kaio oaid chaein teal saaio chao char ter chieeo
cheeao oeao chien cheay daaan kiel oer teaeo kier
sar oeay oeeaeo qiier chiaey tail sieo chiiied
oeen oaaiar kieey chiair teed qeiaio chear tiieio
taair cher seal oen sier oeao chaal oiad oeo chaain
kaaal cheail ched tear chiean cher chael oaiin qeeiy
dian saao chaaan seier qeaao sieaal oeo saen kao
chieo oeaiil sen chiaiy ctheer dieeo oaieed chaeaey
oiir oier qaao oeao dar qal keieed oiy ter chiiin
qiey cheeo sial saaain chiel chaal deeead chaan oaio
chad qead dan oiaiy chaiy chaiiad oiaiiir chil qeiar
qar chio seao saay chaiio oeil oiiid tead sean
dair keay chiel oieo din oaeay ckhiir deeel oied
qen siey dieeo chaieel sio chear kiio chieaay qaaal
chiier kir oeao sied tear seeid qiir chaeay

peir tiaid taain keao chaal oiad oaaiy qeiao qieo
diien oaeay ctheao dio oeean taao cheey oieo oeel
daed dio oeean tiaeo taio chaain seeen tiaeo taio
chaain oiiio deed kiy ter chaaad deel dial sir
qear ter sail oeid diao siin chier kaiio seaay
qar seial cheeiy oair kieio qair chiair tiiiid
cheid dear oiiy chieeo kean chieeen der sail oeid
oio chaan siio chaeaal tey qail saeo qaal chain
daeaaaao chaiin oeiey qaao oaeo qiaiid oer kir
chian qeiao qieo diien siio saaal oir tair chieo
oeid oal kiiey oiiio deed kiy kiio chiao keieed
char qel qied keieed char tiael chied chin saiiy chiy
kiid taal oiad chieer sar sial chieen kaaey oear
qeaal keay oail chey keer diiin qey tael oil
teeay oir seo saaal oir qear kear oeeen keer
daied deal siiy chaead tey qieo oeel daed deeay
tiaeo taio chaain seeid qiir chaeay

shiair taio seiair kaiy oaaed keer diaiy cphaio oeo
chaain qiio saay chaead qir qieo oaeier kaeay
cphiiin tiao chao cheeal chaan chiaiy cphieo oeid oal
kiiey sar cheied dieo chied oied qaeo kiiey ter
sail oeid diao chan saiao tear chil qeiar qar
siiid daaid sin oieio qair sey tiiiid cheid dear
oir oeeed oiil oiey chian oir oaaal oiil chieer
sein sier kir saein oaen cheaan keeo oaeied qen
chiaiy

pio seeiid kiao taan chiar qeao chaed diaeo tiao
kaeo chio sair taio oeo chaain tiy tao chiiaad tear
seiey cphiar qeao chaed diaeo tiao kaeo chio sair
qeiey cthaaan kaear kaeil oair oaio oaid daear
chaiy chieel kiil dien oar chaao oaio daal oair
siey daied chear kaio chad kaien oir oeeed oiil
oiey siey chaio oaid chan sieo saaar kieiy qeiey
cphaar oeaao chiao qieo daid qail oaaio chin saaaiy
dao chaair chaio cheeer kiio cheao deay chaao cheey
chiiio deed kiy qiey dieeo oaieed chaeaey oeo oiel
chiiiil kiao qeeeel qien oir chead chieed cheao dar
kiio sead taio char kiaed cheao oiair cher seay
siail kaid oin seier keo oaaeed kier chiaiy

shiair kaiin teed qiil cheid oio chaan sieo chao
siien tiair tiiil oaeid daaiy teed qiil cheid oio
chaan chiar kaio chiiio oiaid oier qeaao tey qar
cheaay cheeo chiair qeaao saad tieer chiaiy ckhar oeo
chieer cheey chian saeay chaao cheey oiead deaao siin
chieeen der oao chieo cheey oein chier kaear kiel
chaaao seeeel kiy ter oiaed daear oaen saay oiiil
daid tin chiy qaeid daaiy teed qiil cheid deaar
oaeay

piio chiao keid dieo dail sair kaiy oaaed keer
diaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaaan kaear taao chiiad qeil
chaaao saaio chiain sar siead chiiied oeen chao
chieeid oaaaid siin siail qiil cheieed char ter
chiiil daiel dad qair cheaey kir oaeil daael sin
chio seao saay oaaaio dar ter sieao chieeo oen
oieel qiad oen chaeal taal oiaiid oer kir chiey
siio oiil diaeid qey taeal qead dan cher chaaad
cheil oeay sein oear chaied chieeo oeo oeean tieed
deaad sir qar seaao chieaad diaiay cheer oar cheian
sein oieen keeo oiiy diao seian kaeay

shiair tiao seian tiar qaao oiain sied keid
diieiy teey seay seeid qiir chiid kieo oeeiil chad
kiao qiier seiey ckhieo chao siien kiio cheien oead
taed oeeaey qeiey cthaaan qeaio char kiaiy oiel
oiiid tiaiy kead taed oeeaey qar oiel chaed oeio
oeen oeay oear tiaid taain kiid taed oeeeeo char
ter cheal cheiay sear tied qein tiair teal siiio
oaaaal tey qeier teiad daio oeo sin sail oiieo
din oir oaiio seaiid oeieil sin oeail ched kaaio
saaio chan saiil qeaan tiair teed tear tier kaead
qir qail oeeeo chiiiil qay qier oeid chied qaen
teey chian oaiio oeil oiiid tear oeead tail oain
der seay seier qaiil qaal chain oeir kiio cheieo
chey kaid oin chaaen qaad qaid tiay chiey chiair
teal siiio oaio diieo sir qeeen qeio sio saaiy
cher chiiil daiel dad qair oir oaiiiy cheey oer
kaeiay kir oeiel kaid tieaar ched keil chiaead qay
kir oaiio seaiid oeieiy chiair qio siieo kiao kay
keail ched kaaio saaio chan sar saiil qeaan teey
chaed dead kian qar seeel daal oaiir cheey oiio
siiin oiiy oaeo chiaen

shiair qiio seeid kael oen saeo keeel kaaiay qiio
chien chail chey taio oeo chaain kaaan kaeay ctheeo
oiiy diaid taain kaeo chiaen qaay siead diiaay
chiair qiio seeid kael oen saeo keeel kean cher
chaaad cheel cheay oeay oieo saaar kieiy qar siead
chiiied oeen chaiy daao saiar qar seaao siaiiiy
saaaio dar tiaian kir oeaid deeead chaan sio
seaaed chiiied oeen oeiel keel daeaad sir keeo
cheaer ter cheal siiio oaio diieo sir qar oiiio
diieo oead qier kir oiel oiaey chiair kiiieo oir
qail oiieo din chaar oeeiy dian siio saay deeeo
oaad tey qeeiio oiey oear chiaid taain qeo chaed
oiaid oed qein qier taiy cheeeo cheeal chey

pieal oid kiey seal oiel chaay chaid oin seied
qiien kieo oeeao oao char taeal teed qaar tiar
kaead keean keeo oaaed tiay oaeay ctheao dio oeean
qeeen qeier teao chaaio chio sir qiail qaad kiir
qeir tiaid taain taao chiiad qeiy cher seay oaen
qieo saaar keo chied dial sir qar siead chiiied
oeen saeo keeel kaeiar kir chao chieeid oaaaid
siin oieio qair chiaid kaeir kaaal cheail ched tear
oieiy qar chial sid qeao oiial oen oir seo oaio
oiiy deey chier kaear qaio diieo seo oer keiel
kiid teer qar siiid daal daiy chaiel dad qaiy oir
chiel sin sar cheiad dio chan oaied chead kaeo teiay
cheian sein oieen kao sieaad chiiied oeeo chey
qeiey

shiair keeen qaad keel daed cheo siin chiiad taar
daio oeo chaain kaear qiiin teed qiil cheid oio
chaan chiey chiiad taar oeeen taao seaad oiaiiir
oiel chaaao chaaal kan saad kaeieo sair qeial
cheeiy oair keeo saeo qey tieeo cheeao oean chier
teaeo kier chair chieed chied qaeo tieen oeeen
keair qier oead tiaid taaao seeir qeaal tiiiir
cheaid chaeay kay taaan kiid taal oiad dao oeeo
seiey

shiair qiio chad qay kiid taal oiad dan seay seeid
qiir chaeay ctheao dio oeean kaiil qeeiy oeal oiy
tiair qiio chad qay kiid taal oiad dao oeen oaer
qaad kaeieo sair qeiey cthaeil daaey chier taao
saiar qeaao saaio chaio sean chair kieio qair oear
oaiid oaid teio kaiio siiin ter chaail cheao siio
char kao chieo cheey diead daid kear teey chiiaay
chiair qiio chad qay kiid taal oiad dao oeen seiey

shiair kaio oead keao oeean sio siil chiel cheal
oiy keao dio oeean qeier tieo chaid taio chaain
oeo oeel deal sail chey qeiey cphiey chiiad taar
oeeiy chaeil kan sear qaiao tear oaiio oiieo dieo
chey tair chaaan tieel kaaey chiir daio siil cheid
chan siead oeiey tiair taio siil cheid chao sean
dao chaal oail oiel say kir oaaal oail saio dieo
oiaid chian oieel qiad oen chiaiy ckhar seier taao
cheeel oiiio sair qar seier teao chiel chiieo sair
qar siead chiiied oeen oiiy chaao saiar qar seaao
chieed deaao siin cheey siio oieir kair qiad oaid
tein kead tiair ter oao chieo cheey oaay seiein
tiaiy

shiaid qay taao oieo oeao chaeo chaan chiar qiian
kiid kieo oeeaey kail chey taio oeo chaain tian
kiid kieo oeeaey tiaey qaad tair kiel chaed oeio
oaein qiio oieir kaeay cpher sead chier chieed dieeo
say kead tiair tiaiy ckhar siiid daal daiy oair
qar seeen keay cheed oiiey qiad diail tair tiean
cher seay seier keeo saio diao siin oieed cheel
chaiiy kiid qier qeiay kiid taal oiad dao oeen
seeaeo qiier cheaad daal dieey qeal oiy ter oear
chaail oaaaid siio char kaeiy teed kear qiey
diieio taair sio sieaal chaan chiel say kaeay

shiair taio siieo taieel sin oaiy oaaed keer cheao
dio oeean tiao keey oio oiil chiao tein teeel
oier kaear kaaiao keear oeaid chaeay qar chiael
tin chaiy deaeo kier seaao saaio chaio oiy kear
oaiid chieed char ter chiiil chiel oieo oeao cheied
sein cheaal keid chan seaao chieaad diaiay chaiel
dad tiien oiieo din oaeaen qar oaiy chaiy chaaiao
keear chao chaieel sin chair siiel daio oeeo sir
qeiey

shiair kaio oead keao oeean cheed qiil cheid oin
oaiy oaeay ctheao dio oeean qeier tieo chaid taio
chaain saad keel daed cheo siin chaeay keeo oiiy
diiin kaio oead keao oeean cheear qiieo chaed
cheeay kaiiin chir cheal siad chaeo oen oair keeo
cheaer ter chiiin qio siieo kiao kay qaiil taed
oiair cher sein kaio oead keao oeean chieeo cheeao
oean oear oeein teaal keid chan oieio qair chiair
tiiiid chier saay oaiy sier siian kaaal cheail ched
tear chiean chiaiy

shiair taio siil cheid chan sieo oeeao qiil cheid
deaar chiar kiel oaiil qeio qay kaeay ctheao dio
oeean qeier qieo oeel daed din oiid taal oiad
dao oeen seiey cphiel oan saad tair kaid dear
saiid oiil oeaay teaar deed qiil cheid oail oeey
cheeo oiiy daied diaiy keid chied qaen ter seal
oen seain chair sieo oeel daed dio oeean qeeel
oieal oid kier chiaiy

shiair kao siad cheied char teed qiil cheid oio chaan
chiey chiiad taar oeiey ckhieo chao siien qeaal tiiay
cheaid chaeay kay tio oeid daead sir qaad keel
daed cheo siin chaeay keeo oiiy diiin keal seial
chiiio daao oaio chaio oiy kiiieo oir qar chio seao
saay daiy oeo chaed oeao cheied char kieed cheel
chaiiy kir oaear qaeil seo oieo chaear kaiid oaid
teio kaiin siiin qiir deeel oied qen cheey cheed
qiil cheid oio chaan saao sey ter chiair tael oil
teeay chao seaar sar seier keo oeel daed cheo
oeeer oeeaeo qiier seaao saad tieer cheey chiiaay
sead chier sieo oeel daed din oir oaaiao keear
oead kieo oaar kaeay

shiair taio siieo taieel oeen oaer qeeaeo qiier
sied qaio deer kaiy oaaed keer diaiy cphaio oeo
chaain tiar kaaiao keear oead kieo oaar kaaan
qiio chieo dieo chey tieen tio oeid dieo oiaid oer
kaeaen tiaey qaad tair qeaao saad tieer oaeo
qiieo qay kir seiay kiiy seain seiaey qar saay
sein sein kiaid daieo deeay kieiy qeeel daaao
seeid kiair chaeay

shiair tiiil daieo chiiiel chan seay sear teear
kiio cheao deal oiy keao dio oeean taao cheey
chaean tiiil daieo chiiiel chey qeiey cthair qaair
teeo saio oail chey qeeen qaad kaeieo sair qar
siead chiiied oeen chian taao saiar qiiio saio ched
qeiy sein kaio seeid qeiey kear saaao chaaio sied
tair qaay seeiin chial oaeo siil cheieed char tier
kaear kaiio oeaiil chao oier kaaao kiir keeeo
qail sir kieiy tad kiao qeio say kir sid qial
said tein qaaaiy chiiy cheer cheo oaid tial sair
keead kaen qar seeiin oiir chael seio chaal oiaiid
oer qiio saay oiel cheaao oeean qeeen qeiaid tad
teeao oaaan chiio cheien oead taed oeeaey kear
teal cheeed qeiey taid qiad chaaiay qaeay sar oiel
chaed oeio sin oaen keeo cheaer tier kaead keean
qaad keid diiiio oer qier oaead keean qial chel
qaail say kiid kied qeiy cheey chian chaaid qiel
chien deaay oay kiel chaid teao chaio daal dial
saeo qey kao cheao chiiey seiey cthir oeeeo oed qeiy
sein ter chiiil cheel oio chaan chian qaao chaaan
oaen qiiio oiieo chaen qar seaaay oair qail saeil
chey qear tiaid taain keid daaao oiy tiaiy

faieo sir kay qieo oeeao qieaal chaan chiey chiiad
taar oeiey ckhieo chao siien keeo chieed daeaaar sar
chail oaeil deeeo chieed saail siin chieen qiiid
daal daiy chaiy chiiy cheer dieo sid qiair siied
qein teey sieao chieeo oen oaaao kiir kieed cheel
chaiiy kir oeaid deeead chaan chian chaao saiar qar
oiel chaed oeio oeen siaiy taao cheey cheo oeeao
teio tiy kiid kier kiiy oaiio oeieeey saiao tear
oaear kaaaad oiad chier sar siiel chiiied oeeo sir
qar oay qial chel qaaiy sio oaiir chaiy oeo chaed
oeao cheied char kaeil tiiiir oeil chiao oeel
daeaad sir kiel chaed oeio oaein ter chiiin qey
tael oil teeay oiid kier keiey qiio saay chir chiy
keil sal kiiel oiy qaaao chaaio sied tair qeiein
ter chaail oaaaid siio char ter chieeo chaeaaay sein
saiao tear oaear kaaaad oiad chier sar chaao cheey
deeeo chaio cheein teed qiil cheid oio chaan oiid
taal oiad deay seeiy daied diaiy kaear kaiiad
oaid oer kaiio oaaer qaay seao cheied cheio keead
oain chiao qiad chaio dead sir qiead chaey saay
oeaio deaao chan chieeo cheeao oean chier kaead tey
qeaaed dead sir keay dio oeid dieo oiaid oer
kaiio oiieo dien chied oair oael seio chaal oiaiid
oer kair qeied kiy tieeid chied char tiiay chiid
taal oiad deay chaao cheey deeeo chaio daead saeo
taaid chaain kaiiad oaid oer kaear qiel chieo
deeead chaao chien oiead oeiey teey cheed kear tian
saiid dair oieio qair oao seial cheeil chey qaad
teel oien oir chaiy oeial kaaaar dio oeaiid
deeead chaao say kiiy chaiy daiel dad teed teio
tiy qaiiy saeo tiiiil tain oeead kaen teaay oeay
oeaao sieaal oeo sair qar chaao oaio daal oair
chiair tael oil teeay chiar qiel oeao oier kir
chian taid qiaiid oed qein keeo oiiy cheal chaio
chear ter oear oeein taail chean oaio siad oir
qeieir sar seeeo qaar kaear kaiiad oaid oer tian
qiio chieo oiil chier chiao keid dear cheal chad
kiiied oeeo sir tiao qaeiy oiier qaeo qeaao tain
cheaan kiil chael char ter oaio char qaal oeil oer
kaaan qeaio char teey sieao chieeo oen oir seio
kiaey oeein qieao chieeo chiaen qaay seiaed tiay
chaiy daieo deeeo oaen tiiay deeeay chaied deay
seiey

shiair qiio cheial chear teed qiil cheid oio chaan
chiey chiiad taar oeiey ckhieo chao siien tiair qiio
cheial chear teed qiil cheid oio chaan oaeay ctheeo
oiiy oeal oiy kieer chiid taal oiad deay seeiy
cheeo oiiy daied diaiy kaear kaaiao keear chaiy
dao chaieel oeen seaaan diead daid tiao qaeiy
seiay kiid qier qaaaio dar kiio siiin oaiio
oiieo dieo chey keay oeeel daal oaiir sier taiy
chaaiao keear oir oaaan keao chaal oiad oen chiiil
daiel dad qair oir cheeeo oeeao chaid kear teey
chiiiio oeen oieio qair chiair tiiiid cheid dear
oaiio oiieo dieo chey qaad teel oien oir chao
seaar chaao cheey oaad qaio deaad chey qaay chio
seao saay chair keay oeeaeo qiier oeay dieen qar
seaaay oaeiay kir seo oaio oiiy daid qeier qeaao
saad tieer oaied chead qier kaeay

shiair qiieo chaed oiaid oaieer chaaiao keear chiey
chiiad taar daio oeo chaain kaear tao chaieel oeen
seeaeo qiier seiein teed qiil cheid oaiy oaaey
oiead oeiey keeo seao oeao say qaaaio dar qiey
dieeo cheeao oeao chien diead daid kear qaiao tear
oaear kaaaad oiad chier sial oaaaay kaaey oaay
oaiil taiy oaeay ckhiir chaaal oiil chieer oeay
oeeaeo qiier oear qein taied cheeeo say kir chaiy
oeaan qaiir dair sial oaaaay taiy chaaiao keear
oair taaen qiead daaao oiy tieeen diaiy ctheer
daieed oir keay oeeaeo qiier saay oeay deeeao
cher taiy chaaiao keear seil chieo oieo oaeil daio
chaaio oiy tael oaiin kiir cheal chaio oaaaid siin
oaiiy keead kaen keer chaeil kan siead oeiey
tiair tiiiid cheid dear oiiy oead tiair taaad
cheieeer chael oaiin kiir dael seiaid kil taeaar
oiiy chaiy oaaaio dar keeo oiiy oaaao chiid qay
keil chiaead qay taar oeeel daal cheo siin chian
saao chaaan saay seeeed daed oair siir diel oan
seeiy oiey diieio taair oael chad kieo daio daaiy
ter chieeey oiir oiio seeid kan oeead kaen keer
dieeo oaieed chaeaey oiir dael oiiien diiay cheil
chaed dead kiao qeio oan

shiair tiiil chiel oiael sal tan seir keal seial
daeaaar siiio saaad kaeay taio oeo chaain tian
kaiiad dio oaaaao chiaid tein tian keaid deeead
chaan siio oieir kir oaeil daaey sein ter chieeo
cheeao oeao chien deeeao cher qeaid oied tair qar
seeen qeier qieo oead taed cheo oeeer oail oeeeo
chiiiil qay kir chiey siey deal siiio oaaaal tey
teeo saio diiaay cher chiiil cheel deaar oaear qaeo
taed oeao chaaio chear kiio chieaay tiaian kaeay
ckhiir diel saiy oaear kiel cheeao oen oieed cheel
chaiiy taier tael oiiieo daal chaear tiiay dieel
qiey cher seay oay qeiaed tial oain daaao oiil
chiao tein tiar qeaao tey qar seeel oieal oid
kier saiay ter chier keil chaain kaaey oail saeil
chey kaiil taiy oir seo oaio oiiy oey tieeo cheed
kaiir oaeaen qar seeiin cheeeo chain diair teal
saaio chan cheeo oeeen cheey sied keieed char ter
sail oaeil oiier cheaad chiier kiier keeeo saal
oaeo kiair chiiy oaen cheaay chail sieo chiiied oeen
oeeed oiaiiir chaier keal chaio oaaaid siio char
tiaiy ctheer diiaan cheey oaeir kiel cheeao oen
chiaiar cheey oiio chieaay ter oiaid chaaay dieao
oer kaear kiel cheeao oen cher cheear taaao oiil
chiao tein qeaio char kaied chead kaeo teiay oeay
cheaio chiiio dein kaaey chiiiey oeaid chiey cher
cheal cheiay saay chiean chiaiy ckhar sial chieen kiiy
seil oed qaaaio din oaaeey oaeay ckhiir deal siil
chaain sein oear chiaid taain kiid tiao chao oer
taar oeeel chan saair teio keel diieo sen oieed
cheel chaiiy qaiio tear oear qein teal siad dian
kiiy seial qen cheal cheial oiy kead sel qied
keieed char tael oaiin qein oael chad kieo daio
cheier seeiiy seiey cphaar oeeiin seier taao cheeeao
oeiaer tiead daid qeieir oeair kaead tan oeeo
oiiieo diaeid qin seaao chieed cher kir oaiiiy
sail chey qeiey cphaar chail sen chiiay chaen seaao
chieaad cheao chaen qial chieen kaaey chaiil tin chair
qied teeeal say kir chaar chiel chaid teao cheir
daail oaaaid siio char taeal tiaiar chiaiy

fiaed chiiey tier kiier qaao oieo chiel say kaeay
ctheao dio oeean teiad deeay qear teear kiio cheao
deal oiy teey siio siil chiid teal chin chiar qaao
oiaio oiy taeay keeo oiiy chaeil tiiiir chaear
qeao chieo oaid oiiey kaeo qiieo qay tian kaiio
oiieo dien oaeaer oir seo oaio oiiy oein taio
sied qaio chan cheaad daal dieey ter sein tieeo
oaiid chieeo oen oiid taal oiad deay siead oeiey
qeir keein kaio seao chied cheeay keer oaaal chead
kan sein chieeo chael cheeed qair oeaid chiel chaay
oeeaer qaeay seeael qiil cheid dain oeer chaeil
tiiiir oeio taaio tey qaair keeo cheaed qay qaao
oiaio cheio tiy taier kiaid dead chead kiiey qein
teaal tieen kiiy cheer oeaao sial saaad kier
siied qein ter chieeey seeid qiir daaid tian taao
chiiad qen cher sail oeid dieo chiiieeain oead
tiair ter chieeo chaeaaal oen cheaeo kier seier keo
siiay seeen qeier taaan kir oaaeen chian qaead
chied oied qaaiy keiel tiy kir oeaid deeead chaan
chian chaao saiar tair oaen teeo saeao say kir seo
oaio oiiy deey oeeo oiiy deed teao chieed oiy ter
chaid qiad cheao siio oay qaiil taed din oiiy
oaeil daaey siio oieir kir oeaiid dar tian sier
siian qaead chied oied qaaiy kir saao oiaio oiy
qaiao tead chil qeiar qar seeel oieal oid kier
saiay ter oao chieo cheey deiey chiaiy cthiiio diieo
oead qier kaeaen qar saay saad qid tar kao cheao
diair teeo saean cher seay cheaad daal dieey qein
kiio cheien saeid oaaiy kir cheal seao oaaaid siin
sear qier til tey keo oiel cheaao siin cher chiiin
kiiid qeil chian oieel kiil dien oar oeay dio
seao saay chair qeied kiy qiio seio sio chear ter
oieer chaaeen chian qaeid oaaiy tiy kir oaiiiy
chier kaeiay qaao oaeo kier sier taiy deal cheao
oen cher chieeey sear kiaiy saao oed kaeiay kiier
tiey oeeo sial chey keair qiel chiael cheieeer cheey
chian chao chaair oiio chad tiiil chain chiio siiin
oaear kaiio oeaiil chao oier kir oaaao kiir teed
qeiaio chear qein tieeo oaieed chaeaey chiao qaeiy
oiier qaeo qeaao tiiiy chir chao cheal chaio oaaaid
siin oaen teiad deeay teaar chear siio oieir kir
oeao chiiiil qaid oain chaeo teao chaio diaeid qey
qail oaio chiiied oeen oeiel tiy qaiir chiiil daid
tin oaen oeeao said seay oaer tiao keey chaeiy
teed kear taied diaiy kaear kiel chaaao chaaal kan
chaaao kiel oer taiy oeiay kiid qier kaeil daael
say taao saiar keay diaey qaad tair tair chieed
chied qaeo tieen oeeen kieio qiier seaao saaio
chaio oiy ter chaail oaaaid siio char ter chaio siil
cheid chan seeel oieal oid kier oaeiy teed kear
taeay keeo oiiy daied diaiy kaeay

faieo sir qaair qaaio chiiin oeio oaein qeay
cheaay chieo sid tiao qaeiy cheal seao oen oeao dio
oeean qeeiy chaeil kan chiao keeao kiy tiaiy ckhaay
oead taeaad sir tio saaaio dein kir chiey chiair
keeo chead kiao teeay qaair kied oaiid oieo cher
qaao chied keao oer qiel oeao deeead chaan chaaad
cheieeer sein tiiaan chier taiy chieel kiil dien
deaar deear kiaed chiiey tied qeio qay taaio oeid
chiiey taaad cheieeer cher chieeey sear tiaeiy kiio
chiao tear oear qiey oail saio chan sar seaaal chey
tiao qieo chan cheeo sid qeiaey qar seeiin sear
qier taiy daid qied teeeay cher chieeey oeead kaen
keeo oaaed tin oir oeaid deeead chaan chaeay keeo
oiiy oey kieo oail oiel chaaao chear tiaiy

faieo sir kaer teeo saeao say taiy diey chiiad
taar daio oeo chaain qeio taay cheo cheel deio
qaear chiiil dair qiead oeiey tiair kiio cheien
oir chao saal oaeo kiao kiy qein tiaeiy tael
seeeey tail cheey chay qeaao cheio tiy tiel oan
seeel daied diaiy qaiir chiiil daid tin chiy keao
chaal oiad oeo chien oaiil tiao seian tiar kir
chaar oeeen qiaiy taao cheey oeier tieo oaio dead
saaiy kir oeeed oiil oiey oiiiey dieeid oiey
oaieo oain dael seeeey kiir oiieo chaed deeay keo
oaid taiy oaeay ckhiir chieel qiier cheeeay oeaaed
cheay chair saair tieo chaid tin oir seo oaio oiiy
oiey daio sied qaio dieo din saiid oiil oeaay
qar oeer diiin kieed oaair qeier tieo chied oaiid
say kiel cheeao oeo oer taiy oeial kaaaar oeiaey
qeiair tiead daid kear teey cheear teaal teel
oean oaeay chaao cheey deeo chiair qeaaan oeeel
oieal oid kier chian oead tiair ter chieeo chaid
qiad oen chier taiy oaeid oaaiy kair taaen teeo
saean cher chieeo chaeaaal oen cheaeo kier chair oal
kead taeaad sir tair saair tieo chel qiaiid chien
oar seeiin sein oiio siiin oaear qiio saaal
cheeeo oiaey chiiiid cheid dear oir oaiio seaad dar
teey oiio cheao deay chaao cheey chiiio deed kiy ter
chiiaan chian saao chaaan seier tio saean cher
chiiaan chiair teaal kaio chan siail qeieir sar
seaao chieed diiiin oear oeeo sial oaear qiio
saaaio chaeiad teal seier taao cheey cheair qier
seao oaieel tear sar seaaay oiiy chaaid tian taao
chiiad qen cher seay saay chiean oear chaio siil
cheid chao siien tiiil daiel dad qair oaaan qaead
chied oied qaaiy kir oeeal deeead chaao oer kir
oaeil daaey sein qiio oieir keiel tiy kir chiel
sin chiean cher saaao chaaio sied tair tio seao
saay cheiel tiy kir oaear qaao oeao dad tiial
oeio tain chaaao kiel oer kir saiiy saao seier
kiid tiaeao oier qeo oaio oiil dien oaay sear
qiey deaal teel oean chir oiieo chiiieeain chaao
chiiad qen siied qein tiaiy

shiair taaao sied keid daaio qiel chian oiel oiael
sal tan oeao dio oeean qeier qiiio oead taed
oiieo keal sein cheal cheial oil qin seiey ctheiey
qiio saay dao saal oaeo kian kiiil dair qiead
oeiey qio chaael say keeeo oiiid qeiy chiiad qaear
oar oaiy chiar qaaao dieo oaair qiiio saaad tiay
oaear keeeo saal oaeo kiair chaaey oaad keel daed
chiey chaeay keeo oiiy daed dial oiieo din oeaid
chaeay keay oeeil oeaan ter chieeo chieo daael oiao
qaaiy chiieir taao cheey deeeo chaio cheal cheio
keead sir qiiio cheel deio qaear oaad keel daed
chiel chaay deeo sieo cher keid chael saaio chan
siead oeiey ter siey daaao oiil chiao teier chaeiy
kaieo oeao chaaio qaeil sen chiieir tair sieo oeel
daed dio oeean qeeel oieal oid kier cher seay
seier keaio deaao chan cheed qiil cheid oin cheaar
oeay siiel chiiied oeeo sir kear ter chiiin kiio
chaio oir qail oiieo din oir seil chiao seo chiael
cheead chan oear chiaid ker keeeo oiiid qeil chien
oar saay saair qiiio cheel deio qaear deiey oeeo
oiiy chiio oaear tieeen der siey chiio chaio oir
qail oiieo din oaaeen saao chaaan seied kead
taeaad sir tair saair qiiio cheel deio qaear cheer
diiin kaeo taaay tiair qied keieed char taaao
cheeed kaey sar chial oaeo chio oaeil oiier oaid
dear saao oeao dar teal saaio chan seeiin oiir
cheeo oiiy oaaao chiid qay kaeaer oaear qeiar keay
diaed kaen qail oiieo din chaiy oaao chaed oier
taaio oied sied qiial oiy qio sead keay chiao
qiiiy qeeen tieao oer kaear keeeo oiiid qeiy cher
oao chieo cheey chaen keo said kaeay kaid dear
oaaed keer der chiiaan saar chaeier qaao chaaan
seier kead taeaad sir qaay cheeeo chaio deear
taaao cheeed kaey cheeo sieo cher kaeaer oaead
qeiar teio kiiel oiy tael seeeey qeeiy oiey deal
cheied daead sir qeeid kiair chaaal oiiio cheel
deio qaead din oeaid chiey chiial oeio kiiied oeen
oaen chail oeear siio saay daao saiar qeeaer keil
chiiiiy qaar oaao chaaan seied kead taeaad sir
teio kiiel oiy qiir oeao chieo diay cheeel oaaid
kear kear cheear ked teal oiy taaao oiil chiao
teier der seal oen saiiy oaaey cheay dad qail chey
qieo oeeao teial chin oeaid chiey cher chiiaan chian
saao chaaan seied kiy kao cheien chaier kiio siiin
oaear kieel qeao oen chiaiy ckhiail qeieir seeiin
chaao oeil say qiio saaaio chaeiar teal seier
qeaao chieed cher kaeaer chaiy chiel oiaid oeo oer
kir oaiiiy chiair kaeo teial chin oeiey qaao oaeo
qiaiid oer qaeo teeal chey keer oiied qein qaar
oeao oed qen cher siio seeid kan saay chiaey kaer
qiiel chiiied oeeo sir keo oeaiil cheeeaaien
chiieir qeiey cphaar chail cheay seeiy dian chao saal
deeeaaien chiiil diail kaeay cphael seeeey qeeiy
oeaal keay sar chial oaio chaio seeid kan sein
chael oiiien chiao qeeeel qien oaaey chaiil taiy
chaier tael seeeey qeeiy dian oaaal chaeay kaiil
taiy chaar oeeeo qaar kaear kaiio saaad qail
oaaio oen oiid tiao chao oer kir oaiio seaiid
oeieil sin sial cheeean oeeiy oeaal keay sar
seeiio oen saao chaaan seier qeaao sieaal oeo
sair keer oio siied oiel chaay cheay oiiio saaad
tiay oeao oiiir kiiy oaaeey oaear kaaal oail
saeil chel ken chair qiiio oaio dael sier kir chaiy
cheeeo oiiid qeiy oaeil tiiiir chaeier tao oeeil
chey qiead oeiey teey oaeil tiiiir daao chaed oaid
say tiar kaeied tey tail oaid qeeaey qaay chaio
oaaar teeo oiid kaey chieo sir qeiey

peir tiael chiel chaaal oeid oal qid tian kaio chao
chieed oin chaao cheey cheeo oeid dead chey taio oeo
chaain keid chaey seeiy oied keieed seeeen cheiey
oaio oiiieo daead oaeo chin saiid deear tiael
chiel chaaal oeid oal qir teaar deed kiil dain
oiiil dair qiiid chaio oen oaeo chiaen teaad daal
dieey qeal oiy keal seial daey chieo oeid oal qir
teeo sid qeeael qiaiir sio siier seieir oay qeio
oaein qaiay qiey chaeiy teed kear kiaed chiiey
tieeo oaiiy keil chiiiiy qaar oeao chiiad qeil
chien oeeel chiaed chiiey qio sead keay oeair tair
keay chiel oieo din chaar diael qeeeer chaeo qiieo
qay kaear kiid kiiid qaaaid siin oaer qeied
qiien taid qead keay sar chiael tin seio taaio
tey qeier qaad qair qar seier keeo chieer oaiid
chieed char ter seay seier teeo oaeo cheeay tio
saio deeay oir saiir chaiy oaear qaaaid chaed oail
sieo daeaaar oaer qar seeiin cheaad chiier taiy
oeao chaal chil ker kir oiiy oiid kiiid qaaaid
siin siail kay qar chiael tin oeaid chaeay qeier
qieo oeel daed deeay taaan tian qeial kaaaar dio
seao chaal chiy taeal qey ter oeeo chieed daeaaar
cheio keid chan chaaao kiel oer kir oaear
keieeeeiel chaal chil kao oay tiao qaio dieo
oeiaed kay qar chair oead taeaad sid tian kaaal
cheail ched tear oail chan chieeo oaiiy qeain sar
oaiy seio keeey tiar qeo oieo oaaiy chiair teal
saaio chan sar cheeo sid tail oaio chiiied oeen
oiiy oaiio seaay qar cheeo sid qail oaid taiy
oaen sar siead oaiy saiid deear tiair taed oiiio
chey qaair qial saaad qaad kiir taaad cheieeer cher
chaaad dear saay siail kay qar siad oeiy chiiay
oiel chian saeay siead oaiy chaar dio seao saay
chaen cheeo oeeen chiair kaio chad kan cheaar cher
kaeied qir qiead deaao chao chiao sen chieeen chieer
diey chiair taaao cheied chiiied oeen oaiio oeid
oay kir chiey chiair tieeo oed tiaiy tied chen
oaead keean qiead daaao oiy teear tieeo oiaid
oeao oan siiid daeaaar sar oaiy oair qiead deaao
siin cher oao chieo cheey oiaiy qeieir cheao chaiio
chey qied keear oaen cheeo oiid kaey sar cheeo oiid
kaey saay saair teio keel diieo sen oiaiy oeiey
qiio saay daao chaal oaad kiid tien oaay seeiiy
seier qail oeeeo chaied deal oiy qeir tiael chiel
chaaal oeid oeaao ked tey qeiey

faieo sir kaer qeial deal siiil chaal chil ked tey
taio oeo chaain qeio keeey qeiay kiid qier qeio
taaio tey keay oeial deal siiil chaal chil ker kir
seiay teer oeaao siad dey kail chey qar oaen keeo
oead kieo oeeaey qar oeay oiiio oeaiil siin
chieeey cheaar dieeid oaaan der chiiio teer teaad
chiier kaear qeao chaed chil ker kieel kiil dien
cheay cheo oeaiil chan chaaad cheieeer cher seay oeay
oeiael tiao qen chaeal qey ter chiair kiao qiad
oen chaio seeeel kied say kiel chaaao seao chaaio
char kaiid chaeo sair chier kaiio seaay qar oiiil
daid qeean chier keiel tiy kair keay daid chin
oiier qeao chaal chil ker kieel kiil dien oar
seaaay oaeiay qaiiy saeo tiiiil tain cheaay oeay
dieo oeel oal qir qaiil qaal chain der cheeao dao
oeen oaid dear saeay cheeo sid qeiaey qeaao
chieaad diaiay oaear qaio deal siiio sair teio
kaaaal kean cher siio oieir kir sier oaaal cheail
ched tear oaiy oair tiean chaid qeier qial chieen
kaaey chaiil taiy oaen oead tien oied teeeal say
qid kiey siir oeo oaio oiiy oein kieo oail oaaio
chear taeal qey tiaiy

shiair qio seeiid qair chaiy cheao oiiir kaeiad
qaen taaad cheieeer chiaiy cphaio oeo chaain tieel
kaaaar chail chey qeier keo oaaeed kier siey
diaeir tieeen diaiy cpheaar oaaal chead tiien siio
chieo dieo chey qaad kaeieo sair keeo sid kiy
taeal qey qiear qaad qir qeiey

shiair taaao siil oid tieo oaeo chiaen kaiy oaaed
keer diaiy cphaio oeo chaain tieel kaaaar chail chey
kay qiiio oeel ched qeao chiao seian tiar qeao
seaao qay taar oeeen kaen qeaao chieed deaao siin
oieed cheel chaiiy kir oeaid deeead chaan chiair
kaio siil oeaay qeiein taaao siil oir qeier teao
chiel chiieo sair qar siead chiiied oeen oaear qiio
saaal cheeeo oier taao saiar qar seaao chieed
deaao chan chier kead tiair ter oao chieo cheey oear
tiaian kaeay

peir kiaed oiiey tieo oeel oal qid kiy qeay
seiey ckhieo chao siien qeaal tiiiir oaeid oaaiy
qeao chaed chaiio tar taied diaiy taiy oaeid cheier
seaao chieey ter chael seeeey kiir diiil oaeid
oaaiy tao chied qiey siir chaiiiy siio seeid kan
oeead kaen qar seaao saaio chain cheeeo chaio daead
saeo taaid sin oil qaid seaad oiaiiir oir oaaan
kaear taieo oaiio sin oeo oeeao teio tiy kieel
qiad oen cheey cheaal tieen qiio saay chaead keean
qail oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kir oiiy seio keao din
oaiio oeil oiiid tear seaaan oeeen qeio sio
saail siin sar chaaan kir seo oaio oiiy chaio siad
del qaaiy oaied chead qier qaiir chiiil daid tin
oaen oeo oeaiil siin seir ter oaeo taaay qeay
sein oear chaio siil cheid chao siien tiiil daiel
dad qair seaio sean oeio taay oeeen qeier teeo
sieo ched kaael diao kean oeeeo qail sir qar
seaao chieed deaao chan siey dieeo oaiiy kir sieo
daeaaar cheio keid chan saad kaeieo said ken seo
oaio oiiy diaian keaiid dar tian cheaeo kier
seied kead taeaad sir qar seeel daal oaiir
chiaian kir oaiiiy sail chey qiail qead kaieer chir
oaaeen chaiy oiel chieo deeead chaan oiio siiin
chian keaid oiiio chey qaay saair qied tied oair
oiaiy seao oed tied chen oaiio oiieo dien oaeay

peir kaio siil dieeo qid tian qiio saay daao
chaed oaid say qieo chao siien tael seeeey qeeiy
oied keieed chad qaiy saeal oen oeaid chiey oieed
oaair tieo oeel oal qir teaar oeal oiey chiiay
oiio chieo dieo chey qeio oaein qeir qiir chiid
taal oiad deal siiy cheaid chaeay keeo oaaed tiay
oeeeo cheeao oaean siear keo siiio qel qin saay
cheiey oeeo oiiy chiio cheao chaaeey oeeed oiaiiir
oeaid deeead chaan chian oead tiair ter chiiil
daiel dad qair oaen oieiy qar chiael tin chaiy
oeao chaal chil ker qaiid dair siiio cheel deio
qaeo qay kir chaiy cheeeo chaieel oeen seaao saaio
chain cheaad daal dieey ter oaeil kan oeay oeiaey
qeaaal chey qar oaiy chiy taaio cheel oail oiey
chaeay keeo oiiy der oeeo oiiy chiiio oeao chiier
qeao seaao qay qeial kaaaar deeo sieo cher qeaan
keaid chaeay qar cheaar oeay seier keo oeel diieo
oead kieo din chiar kir chao chaal oeieiy saar oeao
oed qen cher sail oeid dian chaao saiar qar seial
cheeil chan saiao tear chiy kaaaad oiad chier seiey
ctheil chiiiiy kiiiey oiieo chaed deeay qeeaer taar
oeeel oieal oid kier chaid qeier kieiy tair sail
saaad tieaal taal oaar kir siio chaeaal kaio chao
sail saeo qaal chain der oiao qeeeel qien oiel
chaaao seao chaaio char ter saaao chaaio sied tair
qar cheeo said taiy chaao saal cheieeeeo oair cher
oaeo teao chaio diaeid qey teey oaeiy tiaead tian
oaaiar kiio siiin oaear qaeo taed din oeieeeed
tial sir qaad keel oio cheeead saio chan sar saad
keel oio cheeead saio chan sail saeo qaal chaio
cheay oeeo cheaer qiio siad chead keao diaer teey
cheed qiil cheid oail oeey daeay keeo oiiy daied
diaiy kaeay

peir taid kal teieo chey tieo oeid oal qid tian
keil saaain chiid qier taiy oaeid oaaiy qeiay
kaeay ctheao dio oeean qied til qaeao chien dieo
oeel oal qir keay diaey qaad tair teiad deeay
qar oaiy siio chael chead tiien cheal siil oaaaid
siin siail kaid oin saay chaio chear kiio siiin
oaear kaeo chiaen qeeael qiil cheid dain seeaer
kaeay cphaeay keeo oiiy chaeil kan seeiy daied
diaiy qiio oaiid oien der oeal seial deaao chao
cheeal chaan oiiieo oir qar oay qieo oeel daed dio
oain deaal teel oean chir oaiio oiid qeaar sar
seier qail oeeeo oeeel qen oeeeo qail sir qar
seier teeo sieo ched kaael diao kean chiiil daiel
dad qair oaen oieiy qeiey cthir oeaiid dar tian
oeid chaey oaean taaao seao cheel dair qeiey

shiair taio sieaad oal kain oio seeiid qair chiar
qeao chaed chil ked tey taio oeo chaain tiy keao
chaid qiad del tieaar oio seeiid qair chiar qeao
chaal chil ker kaeay ckhiir chiid taal oiad deal siiy
oeio taaio tey tieo chied diaen qail oiieo dio
sir qar siiid daal daiy cheer oear teear teaal
teel oean chir oeiey qiio saay chaaan teeeo oeeao
chaid kear ter cheey seay chiean oear chaio siil
cheid chao siien tiiil daiel dad qair oir oaaeen
oaen seieir seier qeaao sieaal oeo sair qar
seeen qeio sio saail siin chaao cheey oaad qaio
deaad chey qar sial oaaaay teer dieo sid tin
oaiiin daar oeaao chieaad diaiay cheer daid cheeay
kaer kal kead taeaad sir qeeen qein oaio seeid
kael oen oir oaiiiy cheey sied keieed char taeal
tied qeaiir oar seeeo qaar kaiiad dio oaaaao
chiaid tein kir cheal siad chaeo chaan chian taao
saiar kaiil qeeeer oeal oiy tiair qio cheaal oiy
taeay keeo oiiy chaeil tiiiir cheaid chaeay tio
oaeil daaey chian cheaeo kier chair chieo chied diaen
qail oiieo dio sir keay dieo oeel oal qir qeeiin
oiiil deear tier taiy chaiio oeaiil chao oier kir
chaiy chieel kiil dien oeeel oieal chead qair seain
sar seeel daal oaiir saaal chiier keiey kaeaer
chaar chiiil daid tin oaen oiiil deear taio sieaad
oay kaeay

shiair qio seeiid qair chiar qaao oeao dar qail
oeel chiiied oeeo chey taio oeo chaain kaear tao
chiiaad tear oaer teeo sieo cher teal siil oaaaid
siio oiy taeay keeo oiiy diiay oeial qen chaied
diaiy kaear kaiio oeil oiiid tead sean cheeeo
oiiid qeiy chaied deal siiy chiier qaeay oeer
diiio teer tieo oeid oal kiiey sar saad qaid
tein kir oeaiid dar tian cheaeo kier oal kead
taeaad sir qiad oeiy seien keeeo chieo oiil chear
ter chieeey sein siied qein qiio seeid kan sar
oiel chaed oeio oeen oeeo cheaer qiir deeel oied
qen siey daaao cheeed kaey sied kaear kir oaiio
oead taed oiair siey chaio siil del tar ter chaaad
cheel cheay oiir dier qiiin ter chier kaeo oeo
oiiel oeen oaaal oiil chieer seiein ter seay sein
oaien sier oeao chaal oiad oeo chaain kaaal cheail
ched tear sar chio seao saay chiiy oair qeied qean
chiel chaaao seeeel kiy tiair teaal teel oean chir
chiey siey deaal teel oean chaiio oeil oiiid tear
sar seeel daal chen chaiy chieel kiil dien oiail
qeieir sar chieo chied diaen qail oiieo dio sir
tiael qeeeer cheeo sial saeao say keaid chaeay
qial chieen taiy dao chiiaad tear chiao qiiiy tieo
sid tin oaaeey chaar oeaao chieaad diaiay cheer
cheeo oaeil oial chear oeaio deaan oir oaaan kiiy
oaear keid daid tin oiier kaaal oeeaey kiil chael
char tiiin qead chier chieo sid kaid oin sar saiil
taair cheear qied keieed chad kaen teiey siey
dieeo chael cheeed qaid chey keo cheel deio qaeo qay
qeil oied kaio chiiio chaeay qeeen qeier qail
oeeeo oeeel qen cheed qeiaio chear qein qail oeid
diao chan siio oieir kir oaeil daaey chier teaeo
kier chair seied tar kaio siil del tar teaal teel
oean daieo oaiio chaaar cheieeeed tial sir tio
seao saay chaeaen tair sail saaad qaiio siio siad
oid tiao qaeo qey ter seay saay chiean oear chiiil
daiel dad qair oir chaiy oeao chaal chil ker tao
saal oaeo kiao kiy qieao chieeo oen oaaiar ter
chiiil cheaiil chan siey oieao chieeo oen oieel kiil
dien oiail qeieir saay saair qeaao sial saaad
kied say kaeiad kaio daair chieeen oiey dieeo
chael cheeed qaid chey tial oaio cheal seao oaaaid
siio sean oier chao seied qeil chaid kay tiao qaio
dieo oeiaed kay keay cheaio deaao seian qier oiid
tiaeao oier kaeay

pead dan cheear teed teao oeo oed qien oeay seial
deal siiil chaed chil ker qieo chao siien qead dan
cheear qio chead qeiey teed teao oeo oed qien oeal
chin oaaey chail chey qeial deal siiil chaal chil ker
taaio seo chiiad qen cheey saar chaaiar kieey siey
chiaid diail keieed saail say taaio seo chiiad qen
chiaiy ctheer dieeey oiir diaeiy tier qiian keil
chiaead qal keieed oiy kaiid chieed char ter chaaad
cheel cheay oiir daio cheeer ter sail oaio chiiied
oeen cheer deaad chiier tair kal kiy qio sail
oieeo chaan oiiy saaaiy cheil oiiid kiid teer
keead kaen tial oaio chao sied qaio dieo oeean
saar oier oeid chied qaen ter oiaen oieer chaiiad
deeen chiel chaaao seao chaaio char tiaiy

shiair kiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen oeeo
oiiy chiio cheieo chey kaer kaen kied saaal chaaio
seeid kaen qeiey ckhieo chao siien tieeo saal chiel
cheay chieo oaio dead sen oaio oaid chaein keeo
oiiy oail oeel chiiied oeeo chey teiad din cheer
chear qeien tao cheal saiio sin oeer chiiiey oio
sead keay sieo saaar qeeel chiaed chiiey qiir
chaiiiy saar oid qial oaeied tey qear keein
kiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen cheey oieed
cheel daed oier taaio oied sied qiial oiy qail
oaio chan oaer kaen taao cheeel oeeao chiiad tey
qeiey

shael oiiien deed kear tiair taed oiaid oer kaiio
oeil sied tear chiaey teiad deeay taio oeo chaain
kaear keid daead sir qeaao sial oaaaal tear chaao
cheey deiad deeay qeeen qear kieey oiio chad tiaey
oir teeo saio diiaay chaaad cheieeer chiiay oeial
qen chier qeil oed qaio cheao cheeay taar dar kiio
oaear kaeo teao oeao sal keieer seeiiy seeel
chiaed chiiey qiir oeil chiao oieel kil tain sear
kear chaeaaaad qeil oer kir sier oiel chaaao seao
chaaio char ter oiaed chan sear kiio chieaal kiao
kiy ter oiaen chier qaiin kaeay

pein taeaaal oen saeid oen chiiad taar daio oeo
chaain tian keaiid oieo chaao say tiar qiian
keieeel sin chio seao saay died oair chiiad taar
oar cheiad din oair qaay oead taeaad sir qiiid
daal daiy oair tieed deaad sir qar sear tiao chao
cheeal chaan chiiin taid qead qein ter chaieed cheao
siio oay qaiil taed din seaad oiied oen oair
qeio sio siier seeen qiaiiiid kael char teed tar
kieed oaair qail oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kir oaear
keil sal kiiel oiy qiio oiil daeaaaao chier saad
qair qaay chair seio sio siier chad tael chead kian
qaay seao diaiy qiel oeao deeead chaan oiaen chian
oaiiad oiaiiir siian qaao oiain saay cheeead oeid
daeaad oeieel kil kan saad qaio dain saad tair
qeio oaein kear chiaead ked kiieo oiaiiir saao
oiaio oiy ter seaal kiaed dieo din

peir qail oeid chiiied oeen saao oiaio oiy keeo
oiiy chiaed chiiey tiaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaaao kiir
tiair qail oeid chiiied oeeo saao oiain sar oeay
dieo chied din saiid oiil oeaay qar chieed daair
chier kaear qeio kiael oan sear qey kaiid chieed
char kao sieaad chiiied oeen oair tiead daid tiao
qaeiy oair qeio sio saail siin sar cheiad din
oair qar seaaay oair tial oaio chao sied qaio
dieo oeeao say qier oeid chied qaen qiear tair
sail oeel daead sir qeiaid kil kao chieo oaaed
kaeay qeiey cphiey chiair kao cheao chiiey oaer qaad
qair qar oaer tieeo kiy ter cheal siiio oaio
diieo sir qar oiiio diieo oead qier kir chao
seaar sar chial oaeo said chaeaaay oaen teeo saean
oieed oier siel chiael oien oail oeel chiiied oeen
oaeay cpher chieeey oiir daid qiad cheier oeead kaen
qeaaed daeaaar chaaen qiiio oaio daal oier qeil
chieo dio oead kieo oeao chaain sear kiio cheao
deay sar cheian sear kieen tiaiy

peir qail oeid chiiied oeeo saao oiaio oiy tiiad
taar oeiey ckhieo chao siien tieeo oied qaen qein
qail oeid chiiied oeeo saao oiain sar siiid daal
daiy oair qieo saaar qar cheio keid diao chan
chaeaay tian keao chaid teao oiey chaied oaaaid
siin sear qiio oieir keiel qiio saay chaaao kiir
kaiin qio siieo kier kiel chaaao seao chaaio char
qaaao chaaio sied tair qeaaay oaen chad tael
seiaid kiy ter sio oaiir oir saein oaen cheaan
keair tiey chaeal qead dan chiair tiaeid kael sean
dao chaal oail oiel say qier cheeeo oeeao chaid
kear tiaiy

faieo sir kay teeead oeid daeaad oeieel kil kan
oaer tieo oeel oal qid kiy keao dio oeean qeeen
qeiaid tio oain chaio siil del tar ter oiaed chan
oiir chaio seeid kaey sar chieo chier chiir cheeo
oiiy oail oeel chiiied oeeo chey qeeen tieo oaaed
tiay chao chaal oail oiel say kir seao chiiad qeil
sin chieed deaad sir qiail qead kaieer cheeed oiil
oiey saar chiiy cheer oaad keel ched qeiy siied
qein tiiin keeo oiiil daaao chear ter chaied
oaaaid siin sear qiio oieir kaiio seaad oaiio
sin sear tier kaiio oiid qeaar sar sial chel
qaaiy oaiid chieed char tiiin teal siiio oaio
diieo sir qaiil taed din chaao saal cheieeeeo oair
oaio chad kao sean oaaaio dar qio oaiir oaeo oeo
oiiel oeen oir oaear keil sal kiiey sail oeeeo
chieo oeeao chiiiin chael seeeey qaay chaao cheey
chiiio deed kiy taio siieo kiael oan seeiiy seiey
cphaar oeeel daal chen oiiy saao chaaan oeay dieo
oeel oal qir qar seaaay oaeiay kaio oeiey keal
chaio oaaaid siin sear keal saiio chaain qaaaid
chaed oiaiid chaeaal tel tin oiid kieo oien oiid
qier kaeied qid qaaio chiiin dieo chaid qiaiir
oiaed chiiey tiiil diaiy kaeay cthaieo sir qeier
teiad deeay qiead chaein kiaiy seiay kiid qier
kaiiil say kiel chaaao seao chaaio chad qay kaeay

shiiil oeil chaid teao oeo oaieer oeao chaal chil
ker qier chaao saal daaiy taio siieo kiael oan
seeid qiir chaeay ctheao dio oeean qeeel chiaed
daead seaiir dieo oeel oal qir tair oeeo cheel
cheeay taier kear cheal chaio oid kiy tier kiier
tao oied tiaiy kiid kieo dio sid tain chaeay
cphiiad taar oaay seeiy oier kiier tao oied tiaiy
teal cheao oen oao chieo oeel daed chiey oeeo oiiil
diieo saio oeier taier teal chaio oid kiy tiaiy

peir teed qiad chaaaao sir tieo oeel oal qir keay
chaiy oaaed keer cheao dio oeean qeier qeeel chiaed
chiiey kaio siil dieen qiio oer keao oiao qeieiy
cheey siio oieir kir oaaan kiiy saeid chaio chan
cheaad daal dieey ter chiiio teer qeier qeeil
oeaao qay qaaaio dar kao cheao chiiey oaer tieeo
kiy ter seir teal siiio oaio diieo sir teao del
qaiin cheal siiio oaio diieo sid qeiey cpheeeo
oeeao chaid kear ter sail oeid diao siin chian
oair qeied qiien teed qiad chaaaao sir teaay chaao
cheey chiiio deed kiy ter cheal cheial oen oair
qeied qiien qiio oieid tey qeaao saad tieer cheey
sio sead keay saad keid diiiio oer qeao chaal chil
ker kaeay

faieo sir qeier qieo seain sied qaio diaiay siio
chien chail chey qeeid qiir oieo chao siien tiiad
taar chaiy chaiy cheao oaien oead kieo oeieiy chiiin
qio chaaao tiy keeeo chaieel sin seaaay cheer
oeiaey kieiy tieo oaaed tiay oir oeeed oiil oiey
oaio chad kaien oeao oiiir kir seo oaio oiiy oaar
chair qeied kiy taied cheeeo say kir oaaal oiil
chieer chiead daid kear keeen keair kaeo oeo oiiel
oeen oir oair qaair qaiil qaal chain daaad cheel
cheay sein chaio cheeer taeal tied qeaiir oar saaio
oio chaan sid qial oaeied tey qiail qead kaieer
chiel chaaao seao chaaio char tiaiy cpheao del qaiio
oeen seiey ctheao chiiad keao chiiiil kan siaiiiid
kael char keeo siaiiiid qay teeeo oeeao chaid kear
kaeo teao chaio diaeid qey ter chiiil cheel oin
saay seiaid qein sier taiy chieel kiil dien chiiil
deear tiair kao cheao diair keay cheo oaiiad oaiin
chier taiy chaiio oeaiil chao oier kir oaaal oiil
chieer oeay oaiil qaal chain oaaal chiier keiel
kaeaer oir oaiiiy chian chaao chiaead kiiey ter
oiaen chian saiio kaeay

shiair qio seeiid qair chiar keeeo chaieel oaein
qeay seeid qiir chaeay ctheao dio oeean tieao oer
kaear tao chiiaad tear oaer qiiio oeaiil seian
tiey siey oio seeiid qair chaier kaiid dan chaeay
keeo oiiy diiil oeao chaed dad kan saay siiid
daal daiy oeao oiiir kaeay ckhiir chaaao kiir qieao
chieeo oen oieel qiad oen chaeaaaad qeil oer kaaao
kiir teed tar kieed oaair qail oeeeo chieo oeeao
chiiiin oieo chil teaao chao chiaiy cthiiio diieo oead
qier kiiy oiid tiiar qier oaeo oeo oeear oir
sier oiier kieel kiil dien deiey oeeo sean dao
sead chear keeo oiiy cheal saiio chaain qaaaid chaed
oiaiid chaeaal tel tin oeiel kaeaer oeaid chiey
cher chaied chaaeey oaear kiieo oaad kiel oiiid
qiel oiaio sean dao chiiaad tear seiey

shaaao oiil chiao teier oein teed teao chaaiay
kaaed keer diaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaaan kaaal
oiead chiiied oeeo sen sio chaaao tiy keeeo cheeao
oaean oaiin qieao chieeo oen oieel qiad oen siieo
chead seio keao cheeay qaao oieo oeeiiy siiio
saead deien oiel chaaao seao chaaio char qiio oiil
deed qiaiiiio dear cheeeo oeeao chaid kear qio
oaiir oir seo chaaio kiel oed qieo dair qaay
seiaid qein saiao tear chil taid qiaiid oer kir
oeeeo saal oaeo kiair oaiid dair oaen qaad qaio
diieiy kiaiy siio chien deeo seaiiy qiiid daal
daiy oair qieo oeaiid chiy qiir oir qieo saaar
keeao siad oaaiy keeed oiaiiir chaar deio keid
diao chan siey daaao oiil chiao tein keao oiiir
tied chen siian kiid kieo oeeaey qieaao oeieiy
chier qiio saay oaao oeao dar qail oeel chiiied
oeeo chey qar siead chiiied oeen oair tair chieeo
kiy tiaiy

faieo sir kaer qeied qiien qiio seeid kaey oaiy
oaaed keer cheao dio oeean qaiil qaal chain oey
qiio seeid kaey seiey ckhiio chieo oiil chier siaiiy
qiey chaio oaid chan chiean chaaad cheel cheay sein
cher oeeo oiiy oio chaael say qaein oaen cheaan
tair sail oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kaeay

shael oiiien deed kear tiair taid qead keal siiio
saead deien oaeiay qeiay kaeay ctheao dio oeean
qeeen qied kaid tail oeeeo cheeao oaean cher sio
oaiir oaen oieiy qar seeeo qaar qaio dieeo
chaeaaay seeael qiil cheid dain seaaay oiiy cheaey
kaeiay kair teeo saio diiaal oer kieel kiil dien
oaay sied kaid tail oeeeo oiaid oaean oieey
chiiay chaen cheaan qeeel oeiey qeaal keay oeer chao
chieo cheey oiiio saead deien oir saio dieeo
chaeaaay chaao cheey chiiio deed kiy ter chieeey sein
oed keil chiaead qay kir saein oaen cheaan qeiey

faieo sir kaer kal tar taio siieo kiael oiiey
kiaid oeil oed tey taio oeo chaain kaear keeeo
chaid teieo oain daid qead keal cheied chael char
qiey chiaiy cheal seied qaaiy chaaad cheieeer chieeo
oaieed chaeaey oiir cheeo oiiy daaao siil del
qeean sail oeel chiiied oeeo chey teiad din cheer
chear tiaid taain tao cheal saiio sin cheaay cheeeay
cheo oeear chaaio oaio oaal oeil oed qen chaied oen
siir oeaad oeaan sear teear teaal teel oean
daier tier qeaad oaiid oer qiel chiael oaaan oail
oeel daeaar oir saeid oen saar oaiid deear taio
cheeer tieeey oiir chieed oaair keeo oaaed teeay
tial oaeo said oaieed chaeaey oiir chieed oaair
qeaao chieey qail oeid dieo chiiieeain oiier teal
siil oaaaid siin seied ken oair qeied qiien teed
qiad chaaaao sir qar chio seao saay cheiey qiio
saay chiel chaaao seao chaaio char taeaaaad qeil oer
qaio oal qieo oaaaiiean saad kaaer qaay oeeo
cheaer kear chiao chao cheeal chaan oed keil oied
qaear oaay chair seao diaiy qiel oeao deeead chaan
siied keay seaaay oaen oeeo oaeied qaaiy kir
oaaeen chaiy oaead dial sid kiy taeal qey ter
oiaen chian saiin keeeo chaal dar kaio chaio chead
tiieir qeiey

feal sein seaal qein qio chaaao tan oiaid oeil
oer qeo oaio oiiy oead chier seeid qiir oieo chao
siien keal sein seaal qein qio chaaao tan oiaid
oeil oer qeo oaio oiiy oead chier seeid qiir oeo
oaio oiiy daio siil cheid chan saiil qaal chain
daeay keeo oiiy der oiaen chian saiin kaeay

peir tieeo oieo oeao chaaio oay keeo seio taed
oeao oiel oiao kay qeeid qiir oieo chao siien
qeaal tiiiir daaio oeid oiiey qeaao cheao chaio
diieo chiy taao oaeo qiad chaio cheal cheio tiy tiel
say kaeay ckhiir chaaan qeao oed qeo chien dieen
qeier qial chel qaaiy sieao chieeo oen oieel qiad
oen chiaiy ctheo oaaed qiil dair keo chieer chaied
oaaaid siin saay oeeo cheaer ter cheed kead teeal
chey keeao said oaeo taio oaaiy tao chieeid oaaaid
siin siaiy taao cheey oied keid chaio sean oaaaio
dar ter saaao chaaio sied tair qar oaiy saiid
deear teal seiaid kiy kieey oiio oaear tiiay
dieel qiey cher chaaad cheel cheay oiir dier kiier
tao chaal oail oiel say kair qeied qiien qiio
cheeer ter chael seeeey kiir ched qeao oed qaail
keieer seiey cphaar dad tael chead kian kiir dier
qiian qeaan qeo oieo oaaiy chiaiy cthiel chaed oeio
sin cheeeay chear qel qay tao cheal saiio sin cheiad
dio chan cher oiaen chian saiin kaeay

shiair teeao chiid teal oen seao sead oeil oan
seeel deal cheial oiy keao dio oeean qeier qaaio
deed qail chan oaio oaid chael chin oaeay cphiiin ter
chaaad cheel cheay sein chaio cheeer qaiay tiiin teey
chaaao saaio cheao oail oaiin saiid oiil oeaay
qaay oeay oail oaear qeil oied kaio cheeil chey
qeeid qiir cheao sied tear chio seao saay cheiey
qiio saay oeao oed qeo chien dieen qar oeo oaaed
qiil dair qeiey ctheaid deeead chaan cheey siio
oieir kiid qal kean chaeay keeo oiiy diael chieeo
chaan seaan taaio oied chiao qieo chiiey qio seaaed
chiiied oeen oiel chaaao seao chaaio char keal chaio
oeo sin cheeead oeid daeaad oeieel kil kan siiio
cheel deio qaen tier taiy oeao oeao dad qay kaiio
oaaed kiy ter sieal oid teeead chaan sier taiy
deal cheao oen oieer chaen cheeo oeid dair tieed
oin chaao oaio daal oair chian cheeel oaair kieio
qair chiair kaiin kao cheao chiiey sar sail saeiy
oaen sear tieeo cheed kaiir oaeay

sheey chiael chied chio seao sead chiel oan oaiy
oaaed keer diaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaaed keer oeay
saay seial dead seao sead oeil oan seeen keeao
siad daaiy taaao cheied chiain saay chaao cheey oaad
qaio deaad chey keeao siad oaaiy keeed oiaiiir
oaiio saio dieo oeeao sel qin seiey cphaier taid
qead keal sied til tan chiaiy ctheair tao oeeeo kal
ken oeaid deeead chaan cheey siio oieir kiid qal
kean chaeay keeo oiiy chiiieo oir keeao said oaeo
taio oaaiy tao chieeid oaaaid siin sail oeeeo
chieo oeeao oer taao saal cheieeeeo oair sio oaiir
oaeay ckhiir diey chiair teal saaio chan oaer tieeo
kiy ter oeeo char kao cheeeao chian qaay cheeead
oeid daeaad oeieel kil kan siail qeieir seiaed
qeao cheear oir oeil chied qiey cheey cheal saaio
chan cheeo oiid kaey sar chaao oaio daal oair cheey
saiao tear oaear qeo oieo oaaiy cheal cheiay sein
chier kaiio oiid qeaar oeair qaeid oeo oeen oear
tiaid taain tao cheal saiio sin oeer cher keao
oiiir kaiiin daaio oeid oiiey keer dieeo oaieed
chaeaey sein sal tead chieed daeaaar chaao cheey
chiel chaal deeead chaao say kir saein oaen cheaan
qeiey

shiair taaao sied keid daaio qiel chian oaaal
deiad cheeal tan seay seeid qiir chaeay ctheao dio
oeean qaay siead diiaay chiair tiiil oaeid daail
qin seiey cthaaan kaaal deiad cheeay kiel oeao dad
qay kir oead kieo oeio oeen oeo chied oier qaio
dieo oail oiel chiieo sair qar oeeao siad oaaiy
kaiio siiio qey taaao cheied chiain sail oeeeo
chieo oeeao oer teeeo oeeao chaid kear qal kaeo
oen oiiy oiel oiaey chiaead tian oir chiey cheey
oaiid cheear oir sier oead taed dieo chey tieeo
kiy teey chian siio saay deeeo oaad tey qaad teel
oeeey oiio cheied oeiey qiir diey chiair teal
saaio chan oaer tieeo kiy ter oeeo chad kao cheeeao
chiao tein kiiy saaaid chaed oiaiid chaeaal tel tin
oeiel kaeaer oaeiad kaio daair chael sead keay
saay sail oiieo din siio saay deeeo oaad tey
qeiey cthir siio chieo oiil chier seier teeeao cher
qeo oieo oaaiy chaeay qaal oeil oer kiel oiaey
chian seao oed qen chier kaiio oiid qeaar oiel
chaair chaeay qaiao tear oaear tao cheal saiio sin
cheiad din oaen sar chial oaeo said chaeaaal oen
sier seaao tey qar seaao saad tieer chael seeeey
qein siieo chead saio daeaal kiel oan seiey cphaar
oeeiiy char taio oead qair chaaio oeid oiiey teian
sein oieen teed kear teed teao chean chian oaiio
sieo chiiied oeeo chey teaay seial dead sen oiaed
oiiey qeiey

shiair tiao seian tiar qeao chaal chil ked tey qaay
saad tair tiao tel tieaad chan cheio kaaaal tear
chaio oeo chaain kaaao kiir kaio siil del tar
kiaid oeil oer kir oiid kieo oeao chaaiay kir
oaiio seaiid oeieiy siey diial del tieaar der
cheal cheial oen oair qeaao saad tieer oao chieo
cheey oiaiy taao cheey oeaao saad tieer cheal siiio
oaio diieo sir tieen keeo cheel oiaiiiio dear
oaeay ckhio oaiir oaeay cphaieed oir qein oiio chiao
keieed char qel kaeir qaiiy oaaal oiiio cheel deio
qaeo kao sean oeaan qeaad oiier oiel chiael cheear
cheer oear tiaeil qair oaiio oiid qeaar sear
tiair tieeo oieo oeean cheey saar cheao chiiiil
qair oiel oied kaio chiiio chaeay qeiey cphaar chiir
daio cheeer tiieir qeeel deal cheial oiy tiaiy
ctheer chiaen chian saiin kiiy oeaid oeeiiy cheio
kaaaal tear seeid qiir chiid kieo oien oiid qier
tiel say qieo daal chil tiiiy oeao chaal chil ker
taier teeao chiid teal siiio saead deien oaeay

shiair taieed cheao siin cheiad deeay kaer taaen
qeial dear chian tiey chiiad taar oeiey ckhieo chao
siien qeir keein taieed cheao siio cheiad cheeay
tiar qiian kaeil oair sio sail sieo chiiied oeen
chian taao saiar tair oeo oaiiad deeead chaan
saiay kael seio cheaal tieeo kean chier qier chao
seaar oieiy keail ched kaain siaiy qeieir sail
oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kaeay cphaeaaaad qeil oer
kaeay ckhiio oiil daeaaaao chier chial oaio dael
sead keay sear kear chiaid taain qeo oieo oaaiy
oieey siio sied qaio dieo oail oiel chiieo saeo
qey tieeey sear teal cheial oiy tier kaead keean
qiad cheeeo say keil oiiid qen sier qiian qaeo
qaaaio chied chin oeid chaey char taio seeeel kier
cheal cheial oiy taaad cheieeer chiiad taar chail sen
chiiay dian keaid diaen teiad deeay qieo oaaaal
tear seiey

shiair kiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen seir
tiael chied siiio oiieo deien oiieo cheo siin
chiaeiy tiaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaaao kiir teey
chiael chied siiio oiieo deien chao seaar sein oiao
qead keay saaio oin seiaey tio seao saay cheiel
kaeaer oaiiad dio oaaaao chiair keo oaaed qiil
dair qeeel daied oaaaid siio cheeay keo oaiiad
deeead chaan siir oir qieo saaar keeao siad daaiy
tieeen oiir chaaan keaiid oieo chaao sey taid qead
qein tiaiy ckhiaiiiid kael char teal siiio oaio
diieo sir qeiey cthaeo oeo oiiel oeen oiid tiiar
qeaid oied tied chen oaear qaaaid chaed oiaiid
chaeaal tel tin oir oaiiiy chian saiid dair chad
tael seiaid kiy tiaiy ckhar cheian sein oieed oaair
teaan qeiey

shiair tiao seian tiar tieel qaeo kiaed dieo din
chiey chiiad taar daio oeo chaain kaaao kiir teey
cheal saaio chan sar oeay deio qiiil qeiaer kaer
taaen qeial dead oain der chaaad cheel cheay sein
cheed teao chaeiy oar seaao chieaad diaiay oaen
oeail ched kaaio saaio chan siiid daair siey oio
siil chiel cheal oiy qaiao tear oaear kaaaad oiad
chier saay cheaar oiio seier qiead dair qiail qead
kaieer oeil oied kaio chiiio dial oain chao chieo
cheey oaay seiaid qein saaal chiier kair kaen
qiead daaao oiy ter oao chieo cheey oiaiy taao
cheey oeaao saad tieer oeeen qar seaaay oaen cheeo
saio oaiy oaeiay taar oeeiy dian saiin qaeio
cheay oeer chao chieo cheey oiail qeieir sieo oeeao
qeeid qay kiel chaaao seao chaaio char tiaiy ckhein
oaeiy kaied chier siad chaaio oiy tieo chaeay tail
sieo chiiied oeen seil oied kaio chiiio chaeay
qeiey

shiair taio siieo taieel oeen oaer keaio deaao
chao chaain kieel qiad oen chiaiy cphaio oeo chaain
kiied kaeiy cheaio deaao chan cheaal keid chan sar
seial cheeiy oaen oieio qair chiair qio chaaey cheey
oael seao oeao chiaead kan oaid dear oair qeied
qiien kiao qeieil chaid kay qeaaed oaal oeo oier
kiiy oaen cheiey oaeo teao dao oeen oeead tail
oain oiir chaaey oein oieed oaair qeio oaein
qeaal keay oeer diiaan chian oir seo oaio oiiy
daeay keeo oiiy dieeo cheed kaiir seaan kiel
chaaao seao chaaio char qiear tieed oin seeiy cheal
chaio oaaaid siio sein chaied diaiy tieel qiey
chiaiy

shiair kiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen chaied
chaaeey siel chieo cheead taio dear chaio oeo chaain
kaaao kiir tiair qio oaid daid kiy kieo oail
oiel chaaao chear kaeo teiay oeo oaeien oiid kiiid
qeo sel qin chio saio dain seiey cthaiio sair kiir
dieeo oaieed chaeaey oiir chaied chead kaeo teiay
seeiin oiir dier kaear qeao chaed chil teeay seo
chaid kead kiiied oeen oaiiiy chian oaeiay kaaed
keer deey chian oieel kaid chaen keead taiy oiiy
chiel chiiiir chaear qieo chieo oeiay daied diaiy
qiel cheaao chan oiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen
chaied chieeo siiid chan chail oaio oiied keao chier
cheed kear teed teao chean sead chier oeeo oead
kieo oail oaaio oiy kieed cheel chaiiy keeeo saal
oaeo kiao kiy tiaiy

peir qiio sied qaio deal seeao say tiey siey
daaao oiil chiao teier diiad taar oeiey ckhieo chao
siien qeay seier qiiio cheel deio qaear dair sieo
sid taael chaain taao oieo oeao chaen sar chair
chieeo kiy kear chiiil cheaid deeead chaao oao siien
qio seaaed chiiied oeen chaar cheeo saead diay
oaear kiel cheeao oen oeal sein oao cheao chiiel
oen oiiy chaid qiaiid oer qeo oieo chaead kian
qeaao siaiiiy saiid deear ter chael sead keal oen
saiiy oeeeo cheeao dian kir oiel oiaey saar chair
qeaao saad tieer chaio cheeer tiiay diel oan oeay
cheeo oead kieo oail oaaio oiy taaao oiil chiao
teio tey tair chieeo kiy ter oear chaed oeao chiel
cheal siiio said kiy taed diay oaaey chaiil tail
oain diaiy

shiair taied oaaao oeio kaeo teieo chey keeo oeel
daed oaid say tiey chiiad taar oeiey ckhieo chao
siien keeo chad kao seial cheeiy oaear qeo oieo
oaaiy oear oaiio qay kir sier oaead qir qial
oaeied tead oiy tiial del tieaar chieey chaed dial
oiieo din oir oaiio seaiid oeieiy chian siel
chiael cheieeer oiio sead taio char keal chaio
oaaaid siin seiey cthir saeo qeaao taio char kieed
oaair qeier tieo chaid qiaiir cher cheal cheiay sein
chier kaiio oiid qeaar sar seeel daal oaiir chaeal
tiaiar chiiil chaiiad oeeao oed qaaiy qeao chaed
chil teeal chaan oao sied kaaaal qaaaid siin oeair
qeao chaed chil teeay chaiel oied tead oiy tiaiy
cpheian sein oieen tiaiy ckhaay siead diiaay chiair
taied oaaao oeio kaeo teieo sir keeo oeel daed
oair siear qeier tieo chied diaen qail oiieo
diaen qeiey

shiair taio chieao oier tao chaal oail oiey seay
seiey ckhieo chao siien tieeo chaed dead kian keo
chied oier keid dieo oail oiel chaaao chear tieeo
oaieed chaeaey oiir diial del tieaar daaad cheel
cheay oiir dier qeaad oaiid oer keeed oiier saay
chieed deaad sir qiail tiean oeeo oiiy oein taed
oeao chiel chiieo said kiy kaiin ter cheed tear ter
chiiil daao chaid teao chaio daal oiaid oer kir
chiey chaeal qead chieer seier tio saio deeay oir
chiey chaeal qead dan siio sied qaio deal seeao
say kaeay cphieeo oier tao sead chear taed oeao
chiel cheal siiio sair qeaao chieaad diaiay cheer
oeeil oal qeaiir oear tieeo cheed kaiir oeiel
qeain chaao sid qaiil taair siey daed oeao chiel
cheal siiio sair qieo saaar qar saad tel qien
oeiey qiio saay oaaaio dar taeaaaad qeil oer kir
oiel oiaey cheey chier kaiio oiid qeaar oaiy oaear
kiel cheeao oen seir kiiieo oid kiy ter chiiil
cheel deaar chaiy cheid dieo oail oiel chaaao chear
kiio siiin oiiy oiel cheeao oen siey oieao diair
kiir daed diay siian keeeo chieo oiil chear tiieir
qeaaay oaen sail saeil chey qear tiaid taain taao
saiad kiy tieeo cheed kaiir oiel cheaao chan chian
oid chey qied keid chan oiiil daid tin chaar dial
oaeo chio oaeil oiier sein oear oaiio qay kair
qeier qeaao sieaal oeo sair qaaio oin sein sail
chair qar cheian sein oieen teey chaied chaaeey
oaear keao oaieo sair keo oeel deal sail chey
tair oaen keo said kaeay qiad chaio deal seeao
oied tey qaad qaio daio oiel chaay chaid dear chaao
chaid teao chiel cheal siiio chey qiiio cheel deio
qaeo qay kaeay

peir tiiil cheeeo saal oaeo kiao teeay tieeo kiy
qiey diiil oaeid oaaiy tao chied qiey oeao dio
oeean qeeeo qaar qaaaio dar ter chieeo cheeao oean
saar dair qeaaed oein qio sail sieo oaid chey
qeieir sail oiieo chen oeiey tiy kiiy oiier qeo
chaed oiaid oer qeao oed tied chen oeeeo oiiir
kaeay cphaied oaaaid siin oeo oaaed qiil dair qaad
tel qien cheer daiy siio saay dian qeo chaed oeao
cheied chad kaen qar siiio cheel deio qaear cheay
chiiil deear qey ter sieal oid qeean saar chair
qeier qiead deaao chan oaiid chieed char ter chieeo
chaed dead kian keay dieen qar cheiad din cheer oar
chial oaeo said cheeao oeao chien deeeay dair chieeo
kiy ter oear siio sied qaio dieo oiil cheied chad
qay kir sier cheeeo oeeao chaid kear ter oiaen
saar oaiid deear kieen tiaiy

faieo sir kaer qeeel chiaed chiiey kaiio qay qieo
chao siien tiael oiiay seaao siad dey keaio deaao
chan cheaal keid chan sar cheiad din oaen chieo sid
qiair chier kiel chaal oiaid oer kir oiid qal kean
oiiieo oir qiaiy taao cheey oar seeil ched kead
qay tao chiiad keel oier kir oiel chaaao seao
chaaio char taaao oiil chiao teier oiey chiiieo oir
qeaao seiair teaad chear oiaed chan oiir oier
kaead keean keo oiel cheaao chan cher oiaen saar
oaiin kaeay

shiair taied oeil chaair cheed qiil cheid oio chaan
chiar qeaao tey taio oeo chaain keaid deeead chaan
sio seeid taal chear qiio oieir qaeid oen saar
chair qail oeel daead sir qaad tel qien oeiey
qiio saay daao chaid kaid chan oiiio diieo oead
qier keiel tied chen chiey chiair taied oeil chaair
cheed qiil cheid oio chaan oeeeo saal oaeo kian
qein saaal chiier kair keay oeaao seeid qay kir
oaaeen cheer oear tiair tiaeil kaeo kier oaiid
chieed char taaad cheel cheay oiir der oaied chead
kaeo teiay siaiiiiail oeel chiiid qeo oeao oier
qaaaiy chaiio seaay qeiey

faieo sir kaer qeeel daaao oiil chiao teio qaaiy
qeaao tey qeiey ckhieo chao siien tiiin tiieio
taair oaiin tieeo chaed chiy teaal keid chan seiey
cpheeeo oeeao chaid kear tiaiy ctheo chieer cheed qeeir
teey oeeo oiiy deed qeeir keal chaio oaaaid siin
seeiin oeay oiail kay tad tael chead kiaid oen
cheey chaied chieeo chiaen qeeel daaao oiil chiao
teio qeo chien deeeao cher kaeay

faieo sir kaer tieeo kiy tiaiy cphaio oeo chaain
keeed oiil oiey oaiin kaio chad taear oaiio
seaiid oeieiy saar chair keay deio tieeo kaaay
oeiey ckhaeo taed oein sead dan siey chao siad chaio
daead sir qaad tel qien oeiey qiio saay oeaio
char kaiin qiio char taeay keeo oiiy oiey chao siad
chaio daead sir taao cheey daaen qeaao cheao chiel
chiieo chey qail saeiy cheer oied keid chan oaiy
oeiey qiio saay chaear kaael chaiin qail oiieo din
oaaeen chaiy oaaaio dar taeay tiaiar chieeey
seaaed cheal sean chiel cheeao oen cher oiiieo oir
taao cheey chiiio deed kiy teal cheial oiy tieeo
oaieed chaeaey cheeeo chaio daio cheio keead chan
seir qel kaeir kir saein oaen cheaan qeiey

shiair kiiied siad cheieed siaiil qal qen seir
kaiio qay qieo chao siien keao daaal tain sail
sieo cher kir oiel chaal oiaid oer kir oaaal oiead
chiiied oeeo saaiy tao chiiad keel oier kaeo oeo
oeear oir oaiio oiid qeaar chio seao saay daiy
dao oied tiaiy qeaan keiel tiy kaeay cthiaen saar
oaiin qid qial said teied chan seiey

shael oiiien deed kear kaio chaio chead teaar saeid
oaaiy qeiay qieo chao siien tael oiiien deed kear
kaio chaio chead qio chaan saeid oaaiy qeiay kaeay
cphiiin kaio chaio chead qio chaan oir oeao chiael
cheaiil siin cher sio chaael say qeaan keiey qiio
saay chiiy oaen chieo sid tin chaaid qiel chien
oeiey cthaiio seaiid oeieiy chier tied chen cheal
seao oaaaid siin sear qier til tey keo oiel
cheaan oir seil chiao oied saaio chaio sean chair
tair chieeo kiy kiaen chier qaiin tao seaar oeair
qier chaid qiaiid oer qaaaid chaed oiaiid chaeaal
tel tin oaeay

peir tiiil oaeid daaiy kiiid qeil oieo oaiid say
taiy diey chiiad taar deaay chieo sid tiao qaeiy
cheal cheao oen chiaiy cphaio oeo chaain kaiio oiieo
dien saaad tial saio deaao siin saiao tear oaear
tao oeeiy saaaio dar kieey oiio oaear tiiay
cheiey qiio saay cheaid chaeay kaiil taiy oeiey
qeio kiaey cheal cheiay oiir dier kaead keean
qeaao saad tieer siear qail oeel daead sir qeiey
cthaiio seaiid oeieiy saar oiio saay daao saiad
kiy qail oeel chiiied oeeo chey teiad din cheer
chear qeien tao cheal saiio oeen chial oaeo said
cheeao oean saar oier seaao tey qar cheian oiir
chieen tiaiy

faieo sir kaer qeeel chiaed chiiey kaiio qay kaeay
ctheao dio oeean qiead chiiied oeen seaan kir seio
taal oaiir siio chad qay kir seaad oiied sean
oaaaio dar taeay keeo oiiy daied oaaaid siin
sein siio oieir kir oeeeo saal oaeo kian kiir
dier taiy chaiio oaaed kiy ter oiaed chan oiir der
oiaen saar oaiin kaeay

shiaid qay tieeo keao oiao teieo chey qiiio oiieo
chaen kaiy chian kaaed keer cheao dio oeean tieed
daair siio cheao chaeo chaan oiel chaal oiaid oed
tey qail oeeeo chieo oeeao oer kiiy oiid qal kean
oeeo oiiy oaaao chiid qay tiey chiair taied cheeeo
say tiar qeo oieo oaaiy cher chaaao oiil chiao
teier oein kear oeein qiio cheao chaeo chaan cheel
dial sir tieo oaaed tiay oir oaiiiy chier kiel
oiael say kair qeied qiien taied cheeeo say kiiy
oeaid oaiio chaaad oen sey qeaao taio cheio qaean
chaaao saaio cheay seiey

shiair teal chieel kaeo kiy taio siieo kiael oan
oaer tio saio deeay sieo chao siien keeo chad kao
seial chiiio diay chiy qeaan qeo oieo oaaiy oear
chiaid ker keil chiaead qaid chey qaay sein siied
keay oal ken saao chied keao chiiiin oeal chaio
oaaaid siin seaao chieaad diaiay chian taiel dad
tiieo oiieo din saiiy chaiy chiel oaiil qeio qay
kir chaiel dad tiien chian qeo oieo oaaiy chier
keaid oaiio sin seaao saad tieer chieeey sein
chieeo oaeo tead kian qail saeil chey qeeel daal
cheo siin oaien oiiy oaiio oiid qeaar saaio oio
chaan oaen chio oeaiid deeead chaao oaeo kiy tiaiy
ckhar oaiy oaen cheeo oeeen saaao chaaio sied tair
keair kaeaer saaaid chaed oiaiid chaeaal tel tin
oir oiieo chen oaen chad tael seiaid kiy tieo oean
chaaey cheay oail seaal tiao tey qiail kay tio
seao chaieel oan saay siead deaao seian kiel
oaiil qeio qay tiar qeo oieo oaaiy oael seao
cheil oiiir kaen cheeo oeid dair kaen qaad tied
oeio oaein qiir diel chiiiir chaen chial oaio chaio
seeid kiiey kiao qeeeel qeeey cheeo seao oieo
daear oaeay

peir teal chieel kaeo kiy keao dio oeean qiiid
daal daiy oeao chaieed dey qail seaal tiao tey
tair seaao siad del qiien teaal keid chan sail
oeeeo chieo oeeao oer qiel chiael oaaao oail oeel
daeaar saiiy saao seier taao saal cheieeeeo oair
saaao chaaio sied tair keo chieer chiaiy ckheio sio
siier sar sail sieo cher kiid tiiar qiio chien
chiiio deed kiy kao sieaad chiiied oeeo chey keay
oail seaal tiao tey qeeel daal oaiir cheey chiiil
cheil chiaead qay taaio oeid oiiey qiail qeieir
cheian sein oieen taio sieaad oay qaaad tial saio
deaao chan seay seeid qiir chaeay

peir kaio chad kiiey teal chieel qay kaeay ctheao
dio oeean kaer tieo sid keeay kiel oaiil qeio
qay kaeay ckheay oeay oeeid qiir oeo oaio oiiy
oiey dier keil chiiad qaead oiaiid chien deio tied
oien saao oaeo kier cheey chaeaay taiy chiel oieo
